- **Done (continued)**: #369 (4-Week Billing/Duration Option: investigation found Membership Plans already support an arbitrary 4-week period today via the existing generic `billing_policies.recurring_billing_interval`/`recurring_billing_unit` fields — `advanceBillingDate()` in `billing.ts` already advances `unit='week'` by `interval * 7` days, so interval=4/unit='week' correctly yields 28 days and is never conflated with a calendar month; no Plan-side schema or code change was needed. Products' `billing_frequency` had no such interval multiplier — it's a fixed single-value enum used only as a descriptive label (actual entitlement length is tracked separately via `validity_days`) — so migration 123 widens the `gym_charges_billing_frequency_check` CHECK constraint to add `'four_weeks'` alongside the existing `once/per_session/week/month/year` (renumbered from 122 to 123 after merging #370, which independently claimed migration 122 for `enrollment_status`); `VALID_FREQUENCIES` in `sellable-items.ts` and `FREQUENCIES` in the admin Products page updated to match; `frequency_four_weeks` i18n key added to en/es/ca under the `sellable_items` namespace; `advanceBillingDate` exported and covered by a new unit-test file `billing-date-math.test.ts` (5 tests, no DB) confirming 4×week = 28 days and that it never equals a 1-month advance; `gym-charges.test.ts` extended with POST/PUT `billing_frequency=four_weeks` acceptance tests and an invalid-value rejection test).
- **Done (continued)**: #371 (System Product — Personal Training Class Package (10 Sessions): data/setup-only migration 124 backfills one system Product per existing gym into `gym_charges` — `type='sessions'`, `units=10`, `is_system=1`, `status='active'`, `enrollment_status='staff_only'` (maps the ticket's "Active — Only Internal" onto the split introduced by #370: staff can sell it internally, Members cannot purchase it directly), `validity_days=182` (≈6 calendar months — `gym_charges` has no month-based validity field, only a fixed day count), `tax_rate_id` set to each gym's own system default tax rate (same lookup migration 113 used); `amount` left `NULL` so price stays configurable via the normal Product admin flow; no API, UI, or model changes — `gym_charges.name` has no localization mechanism today so no translated-name column was added; idempotent via `NOT EXISTS` guard, `down` deletes the seeded rows; migration renumbered from 123 to 124 after merging #369, which independently claimed migration 123 for the four-week billing frequency).
- **Done (continued)**: #370 amendment (Align Plan Enrollment Status with Products: migration 125 removes `closed` from `membership_plans.enrollment_status` — only `public`/`staff_only` remain, mirroring `gym_charges.enrollment_status`; existing `closed` plans migrated to `public` per the ticket's explicit mapping; column default changed from `closed` to `staff_only`; new plans, duplicated plans, archived plans, and soft-deleted plans now default/reset to `staff_only` instead of `closed`; `VALID_ENROLLMENT`/`ENROLLMENT_STATUSES` in `membership-plans.ts` and the admin Plans page drop `closed`; `membership-plans.test.ts` and the plan fixtures in `billing-run.test.ts`/`payment-page.test.ts`/`payment-requests.test.ts`/`payments-billing-events.test.ts`/`plan-charge-benefits.test.ts`/`recycle-bin.test.ts` updated to use `staff_only`).
- **Done (continued)**: #388 (Inline Tax Management, Descriptions, Audit Information & Impact Warning: migration 126 (renumbered from 125 after merging #370's amendment, which independently claimed migration 125 for the `enrollment_status` change) adds `tax_rates.description` (nullable) plus `created_by_name`/`created_by_type` and `modified_by_name`/`modified_by_type` — `created_by_type`/`modified_by_type` is a CHECK-constrained `'staff'`/`'superadmin'` column snapshotting the actor at write time, fixing a real bug where `created_by_membership_id`/`modified_by_membership_id` (joined to `gym_memberships` for display) silently showed no actor at all for a superadmin acting directly, since superadmins have no `gym_memberships` row; backfilled for existing rows resolvable via the old membership-id join, left unattributed otherwise; `POST /taxes`, `PUT /taxes/:id`, `POST /taxes/:id/activate` and `POST /taxes/:id/deactivate` all populate the new columns going forward. `PUT /taxes/:id` now computes the current count of non-deleted `gym_charges` (Products) and `membership_plans` referencing the tax before persisting an edit — Membership instances are deliberately not counted, since Taxes are never snapshotted onto a Membership at instantiation; if either count is non-zero and the request omits `confirmImpact: true`, it returns `409 { error: 'confirmation_required', impact }` without writing, so a direct API call can't bypass the warning. Admin Taxes page (`apps/admin/.../financials/taxes/`) rebuilt to match the `sellable-items` page's standard pattern — inline "+ Add" row and inline row-level Edit (no create/edit modal), expand/collapse row with the description truncated in the collapsed header and shown in full when expanded, Details modal shows the actor's Staff/Superadmin label; Save on Edit now surfaces the impact warning as a confirm dialog (with the affected counts) before resubmitting with `confirmImpact: true`. New `api/src/test/taxes.test.ts` (this router had no prior test file) covers tenant isolation, auth/role gating, the confirmation-required flow (both triggered and skipped), audit actor population for staff vs. superadmin, is_system delete protection, and soft-delete visibility.).
- **Done (continued)**: #367 (Member Web Feature Flags — sub-issue of #362: extends the `feature_flags` infrastructure with a `member_web.*` namespace, separate from the Admin App's own flags. Migration 127 seeds `member_web.my_training_plan`/`my_nutrition`/`my_bookings`/`my_membership`/`profile`, all `enabled=1`. Backend: each flag additionally gates the `/me/*` endpoints specific to that section (chained after any existing flag on the same route); superadmins bypass unconditionally, including while impersonating a member. `GET /me/schedule` (shared with the un-flagged `/calendar` page), `GET /me/payment-requests` (shared between `/membership` and `/profile`), and `GET /me/profile` (the app's core `isLinked` bootstrap call) are deliberately left ungated at the API layer to avoid one section's flag blocking another — those three rely on the frontend route guard alone. Frontend: new `apps/member/src/context/FeatureFlagsContext.tsx` (mirrors the admin app's), Home hides the disabled nav tiles/My Membership card, and each of `/training`/`/nutrition`/`/schedule`/`/membership`/`/profile` redirects to Home when its flag is off for a non-superadmin. The existing generic Admin Feature Flags tree UI needed no changes — it renders `member_web` as its own section automatically. New `api/src/test/member-web-feature-flags.test.ts`).
- **#362 (in progress — Slice 1 of Option A)**: Members App audit — fixed Home's Next Booking / Membership sections staying stuck on `Loading...` forever under admin impersonation. Root cause was two compounding bugs: (1) `apps/member/src/app/api/proxy/[...path]/route.ts` stripped the `x-impersonate-as`/`x-center-id` headers before forwarding requests to the API, so the backend never saw the impersonation target even though `tenantContext`/`resolveMemberId` already handled it correctly; (2) `AppContext`'s data-loading effect only depended on `[isSignedIn, user?.id]` and read `sessionStorage` once inline, so it never re-ran when impersonation started/stopped, leaving `isLinked` stuck at its pre-impersonation value forever. Fixed the proxy allow-list, reordered `ImpersonationProvider`/`AppProvider` nesting so `AppContext` consumes `useImpersonation()` reactively, and added `x-impersonate-as` to every `/me/*` call `AppContext` makes (centers, notifications count). Added `api/src/test/me-impersonation-context.test.ts` covering `/me/schedule` and `/me/membership` under impersonation. Calendar, Bookings, Training, Nutrition, Payments, Alerts, and Profile audits remain as follow-up PRs against #362.
- **#362 (in progress — Slice 2 of Option A)**: found and fixed a related latent bug left over after Slice 1: `centerContext` (the middleware resolving a `member` role's assigned centers, runs after `tenantContext` on every `/me/*` route) matched `member_centers` against the impersonating superadmin's raw `userId`/`clerk_user_id` instead of the effective identity, so any `/me/*` request carrying `x-center-id` while impersonating a member with `member_centers` rows always resolved to zero allowed centers and 403'd — silently, since `page.tsx`'s outer `catch` degrades to empty state rather than surfacing the error. `centerContext` now mirrors `resolveMemberId`'s `effectiveType`-based branch: `members.id = effectiveUserId` under member impersonation, `members.clerk_user_id = userId` otherwise. Extended `api/src/test/me-impersonation-context.test.ts` with center-scoped impersonation cases (own center allowed, other center 403).
- **#782 (in progress — repo half done; Grafana half pending)**: freshness alert for the nightly runs. New unauthenticated `GET /health/runs` (outside `/billing/`, so the nginx GitHub Actions allowlist never blocks the prober) answers `{ billing, recurring_bookings }` with `{ last_completed_at, age_hours, stale }` each, from the latest `completed` row of each run log; threshold `RUN_FRESHNESS_THRESHOLD_HOURS` (default 26, `domain/runFreshness.ts`). No migration. Remaining: the owner configures the Grafana Cloud synthetic check, alert rule and contact point by hand — steps in `docs/go-to-production.md` §4b and `docs/payments.md` → Observability today.
- **Done (continued)**: #350 (Nutrition Library — flat list, expandable rows, search + multi-select filters: replaced the category-grouped layout on both `/cordel/nutrition-library` and `/nutrition/nutrition-library` with a flat, paginated `DataTable` list ordered by name; new `MultiSelectFilter` component (`apps/admin/src/components/MultiSelectFilter.tsx`) provides Excel-like checkbox dropdowns for Category (OR semantics) and Nutrition Properties (AND semantics — item must have every selected quality), combined with a debounced name search and an existing-pattern prev/next pager. Shared query-building/pagination helpers extracted to `api/src/domain/nutritionLibrary.ts` (`buildListWhere`, `loadQualitiesMap`, `replaceQualities`, `validateQualityIds`, `clampLimit`/`clampOffset`) and reused by both `platform-nutrition-library.ts` and `nutrition-library.ts`; `GET` on both routers now returns `{ items, total, limit, offset }` (was a bare array) and accepts `?search=`, repeatable `?category=`, repeatable `?quality_id=`, `?limit=`/`?offset=`. Per the issue's answered clarifying questions: the gym-facing router gained `POST /nutrition-library` and `PUT /nutrition-library/:id` (both `requireModuleWrite('NUTRITION')`) so gym staff with write access can create and edit their own gym-owned items (`gym_id` = their gym); system items (`gym_id IS NULL`, created via `/cordel/nutrition-library`) stay read-only there — editing one returns 403, editing another gym's item returns 404. Nutrition-data-quality review (data model stayed qualitative-only per the issue's Q2 answer — no numeric columns added): migration 127 backfills the `protein`/`carbohydrate` quality tags for the unambiguous seeded system items (chicken/turkey/salmon/etc. → protein; rice/potatoes/oats/etc. → carbohydrate), since the original #163/#293 seed data had never been tagged. Tests extended in `platform-nutrition-library.test.ts` and `nutrition-library.test.ts` for search/filter/pagination, the gym-write permission boundary, and tenant isolation on edit.).
- **Done (continued)**: #410 (Financials — Assigned Plans view: new read-only `GET /user-memberships` field `lifecycle_status` — a date-aware projection of the stored `status` column computed in SQL (`pending` when `starts_at` is in the future, `expired` when an otherwise-active row's `ends_at` has passed, `active`/`paused`/`cancelled`/the explicit `expired` value passed through unchanged otherwise); no migration, no change to the stored `status` values or existing business logic. New admin page `financials/assigned-plans` lists every Membership assignment across the gym (Member Name/Plan Name/Start Date/End Date — "Open-ended" when `ends_at` is null/Status badge), reusing the existing `/user-memberships` endpoint; nav entry added to the Financials group (`financials.assigned_plans` feature key, defaults enabled — not yet seeded in `feature_flags`); `status.pending` i18n key added alongside the existing `status.*` badge labels in en/es/ca. `user-memberships.test.ts` extended with `lifecycle_status` coverage for all five states.
- **Done (continued)**: #411 (Financials — advanced filtering on the Assigned Plans view, building on #410's `lifecycle_status` field. `GET /user-memberships` gains three additive, backward-compatible filters: repeatable/comma-separated `lifecycle_status` (multi-select over `pending`/`active`/`paused`/`expired`/`cancelled` — filtered via an outer query over a derived table, since `lifecycle_status` is a `CASE`-expression SELECT alias and can't be reused directly in the same query's `WHERE`), and `start_date`/`end_date` (date-range overlap against `starts_at`/`ends_at`). The existing single-value `status` param (raw stored column) and `member_id` are unchanged — `lifecycle_status` is a separate param rather than repurposing `status`, since `pending` has no equivalent raw stored value and existing callers (`memberships/page.tsx`, `MemberPaymentsModal.tsx`) rely on `status` matching the raw column. `financials/assigned-plans` page gets a filter bar: a `MultiSelectFilter` (existing component, #350) for status, a debounced member-search filter (mirroring `payments/billing-events`' inline `MemberFilter`), and `start`/`end` date inputs, with a "Clear filters" action and a distinct empty-state message when filters exclude all rows. New `filter_status`/`filter_member`/`filter_from`/`filter_to`/`filter_clear`/`no_matches` i18n keys in en/es/ca. `user-memberships.test.ts` extended with coverage for single/multi/comma-separated `lifecycle_status` filtering, invalid-value 400s, combining `member_id` with `lifecycle_status`, the date-range overlap semantics, and malformed-date 400s.).
- **Done (continued)**: #412 (Members — plan history sorting + Cancel/Assign New Plan transition workflows; no migration, existing `status` enum already had `cancelled`/`expired`). New `POST /user-memberships/:id/assign-new-plan` (admin-only, mirrors the admin-only gate on the existing cancel `DELETE`) atomically supersedes a Membership: in one transaction it flips the current row to `expired` only if it's still `active`/`paused` (a row already `cancelled`/`expired` is left untouched), inserts the new `active` `user_memberships` row for the same Member via the existing `effectivePrice()` snapshot logic (same discount-override/`discount_reason` validation as `POST /`), and adds the owner to `user_membership_members` — avoiding the `active_member_key` unique-index collision by expiring the old row before inserting the new one within the same transaction (that index is per-(Member, Plan) since #634 / migration 172, and the ordering still matters for a same-Plan supersede). Admin `MemberExpandedRow.tsx` (Members page) now lists a Member's **full** plan history (the `GET /user-memberships?member_id=` list was already sorted `starts_at DESC` from #410/#411) instead of only the latest row, with a per-row admin-only `ContextMenu`: "Cancel Plan" (reuses the existing admin-only `DELETE /user-memberships/:id`) and "Assign New Plan" (new `AssignNewPlanModal.tsx`, plan-picker + start date, calling the new endpoint). Benefits/next-billing-date display stay scoped to the most recent row. New locale keys in `members.*` (en complete; es/ca only for the new keys, since `members.*` in those two locales was already significantly behind en before this change). `user-memberships.test.ts` extended with tenant-isolation/auth/happy-path/invariant/validation coverage for the new endpoint.
- **Done (continued)**: #413 (Membership Plans — applicable tax, aligned with Products' financial config: `membership_plans.tax_rate_id FK→tax_rates` was already added in #311's migration 112/113 and backfilled to each gym's system tax rate, but no `tax_behavior` column existed alongside it and neither field was ever surfaced by any endpoint or the admin UI — `taxes.ts`'s `getTaxImpact()` already counted `membership_plans` referencing a tax rate, confirming the field was meant to be live. This ticket wires it up rather than duplicating Plans' existing price (`membership_plan_prices`, a validity-windowed price history) or renewal-frequency (`billing_policies.recurring_billing_interval`/`recurring_billing_unit`, already `day`/`week`/`month`/`year` — no `once` option, so "excluding one-shot" already held) mechanisms, which are richer than Products' flat `amount`/`billing_frequency` and are already exposed in `enrichPlan`/the Plans page's Billing and Prices sections. Migration 135 adds `membership_plans.tax_behavior VARCHAR(20) CHECK ('inclusive','exclusive') DEFAULT 'inclusive'`, mirroring `gym_charges.tax_behavior` from #311 (column add and CHECK constraint guarded independently per `126_tax_rates_description_and_audit_actor.js`'s pattern, so a retry after a partial failure can resume safely). `sellable-items.ts` now exports `computePriceFields`/`validateTaxRateId` for reuse. `membership-plans.ts`: POST validates `tax_rate_id`/`tax_behavior` and defaults `tax_rate_id` to the gym's system tax rate when omitted (mirroring the #311 backfill), PUT updates both fields, duplicate copies them; `enrichPlan` LEFT JOINs `tax_rates` and reuses `computePriceFields` against the plan's `current_price` to return `tax_rate_name`/`tax_rate_percent`/`amount_excl_tax`/`amount_incl_tax`. Admin Plans page (`plans/page.tsx`): Tax Rate + Tax Behavior selects added to the inline create and edit forms (fetches `GET /taxes`, same `taxRateOptions()` pattern as the Products page); expanded Details' Prices section shows the resolved tax rate and the excl/incl-tax breakdown of the current price. New `Applicable tax on membership plans` describe block in `membership-plans.test.ts` (system-tax-rate default, explicit tax rate + computed fields, cross-gym tax_rate_id 400, invalid tax_behavior 400, PUT update, price-with-tax computation, duplicate copies tax fields).
- **Done (continued)**: #479 (Allow changing Center and Trainer on Calendar Events — Trainer editing already existed end-to-end; only `center_id` was missing. `calendarEventsRouter`'s `EVENT_SELECT` gains a `LEFT JOIN centers` for `center_name`; `POST`/`PUT /calendar-events` accept `center_id` independently of `space_id`/`trainer_membership_id` via the same `IF('center_id' in req.body, ?, center_id)` idiom already used for those fields; a new `isValidCenterId()` helper rejects a `center_id` outside the caller's gym with 400 — `resolveCenterId()`/`centerContext` aren't used here since `calendarEventsRouter` isn't mounted with the `centerContext` middleware (unlike `classSessionsRouter`), and unlike sessions a plain event's center stays optional with no default auto-assignment and no conflict rule. No migration — `calendar_events.center_id` already existed (migration 134). Admin `EventDetailsPanel`/`CalendarEventModal` (`CalendarEventForm`, `EMPTY_FORM`) gain a `center_id` field and a Center `<select>` next to Trainer/Space; `page.tsx` fetches `GET /centers` in its own non-fatal call (kept out of the page's required `Promise.all` since `organization.centers` is a feature flag some gyms don't have enabled) and threads `center_id` through `openEdit`/`handleSave`. `event_center` i18n key added to en/es/ca. `calendar-events.test.ts` extended with 5 tests: create/read with center, cross-gym center_id 400 on POST and PUT, set/clear center_id independently, and updating trainer+center together).
- **Done (continued)**: #504 (Fix Admin Staff User Impersonation — the shared `GET /platform/impersonation/targets` endpoint always returned both staff and members with no way to filter, so the Admin app's `ImpersonationDialog` showed Members mixed in among Staff even though it only ever wanted Staff; added an optional `type=staff|member` query filter, enforced server-side, defaulting to the original combined behavior when omitted so the Member app's `MemberImpersonationDialog` — which legitimately needs `type=member` via the same endpoint — is unaffected; Admin's dialog now always requests `type=staff`. Separately, the Team page's own per-row Impersonate button was silently broken — it POSTed `/platform/impersonation/:targetId` without the required `targetType` body field, so every attempt 400'd; fixed by sending `targetType: 'staff'`. Nameless staff (granted via an existing Clerk user, no name/email) fall back to their raw Clerk user ID as `name` for search matching — the dialog now detects that fallback and shows a translated "Unnamed staff member" placeholder instead of the technical ID, plus email as secondary disambiguating info when available. No migration. `impersonation.test.ts` extended with 3 tests for the new `type` filter. **Follow-up (reopened):** the ticket persisted after the above landed — root cause was a frontend race in `ImpersonationDialog`'s two separate search-triggering `useEffect`s (one immediate on mount/gym-change, one debounced per keystroke) with no guard against out-of-order responses, so a slower unfiltered request could resolve after a faster filtered one and silently overwrite it, making search appear broken and showing unrelated staff. Fixed by consolidating into a single effect with a request-sequence guard (`useRef` counter) that only ever applies the most recent response. Backend `gym_id` filtering was re-verified correct and locked in with a new cross-gym isolation test on `GET /targets`; no backend change was needed). **Follow-up (reopened again):** still showing wrong/unnamed staff after the above. Root cause was in `gym-users.ts`, not `impersonation.ts`: `POST /gym-users`'s "existing Clerk user" grant path (the common case — the admin picks a Team role for a user Clerk already knows) inserted only `user_id, gym_id, role` into `gym_memberships`, silently dropping the `name` the admin had just typed into the Add Team Member form. That row then had no `name` *or* `email`, so `impersonation.ts`'s existing `COALESCE(gm.name, gm.email, gm.user_id)` fallback (from #364) resolved to the raw Clerk user ID for every search and display — exactly the "technical-looking identifiers instead of the real Staff member" symptom reported. Fixed by persisting `name` on both the insert and the re-grant/role-change update paths (`COALESCE(?, name)` on update, so re-granting a different role without retyping the name never clobbers one saved earlier). Also fixed a related i18n gap while in this dialog: `ImpersonationDialog` was rendering the raw `role` enum value (e.g. `front_desk`) as secondary text instead of a translated label — added `role_*` keys to the `impersonation` namespace in en/es/ca (mirroring `team`'s existing `role_*` keys) and switched the dialog to use them. New `api/src/test/gym-users.test.ts` (this router's first test file — auth/role guards, tenant isolation on `GET /gym-users`, and the name-persistence regression for both the fresh-grant and re-grant paths, plus the existing new-invitation path for contrast).
- **Done (continued)**: #592 (Link Staff records to auth identities; Impersonate lists Staff; remove Team — root cause of the empty Admin Impersonate dialog: `GET /platform/impersonation/targets?type=staff` reads `gym_memberships`, but the Staff page reads the `staff` table and nothing ever wrote `staff.gym_membership_id` (present since #123), while the only UI that created `gym_memberships` rows — the Team page — had been dropped from navigation in #77 and was orphaned. No migration. `api/src/infra/staff-access.ts` now holds the Clerk invite/grant/revoke/link mechanics moved from the deleted `gym-users.ts`; `POST /staff` grants access on create with the role fixed by `PROFILE_ROLE_MAP` (profile → RBAC role, `infra/permissions.ts`, mirrored in `apps/admin/src/config/permissions.ts`) and stores the link; `PUT` syncs role/name (self + last-admin guards); deactivate revokes the login (Clerk user kept); delete revokes and removes the Clerk user when it was their last gym; new `POST/DELETE /staff/:id/access` for retry/resend/revoke; `POST /staff/link` replaces `/gym-users/link` (`GymContext` self-heal repointed; webhook import repointed). Impersonation staff targets are `gym_memberships LEFT JOIN staff`, returning the Staff name + `profile`; gym-owner admin rows (no Staff record) stay impersonable. Admin: `/team` + `/link-team` pages and the `team` i18n namespace removed; Staff row's Clerk section became **App access** (status, role, Send/Resend invitation, Revoke) with a role hint under the Profile select; `ImpersonationDialog` shows the profile. Tests: new `staff-access.test.ts` (auth/tenant guards, grant-existing/invite paths, unknown profile 400, inactive-on-create, Clerk failure keeps the row, resend overwrites `invitation_id`, PUT role/name sync, last-admin + self guards, deactivate/delete/revoke semantics), `impersonation.test.ts` extended for the join, `require-role.test.ts` retargeted to `/staff`, `gym-users.test.ts` deleted.)
- **Done (continued)**: #594 (Refuse member/staff email collision within a gym — surfaced while verifying #592: granting staff access to an email that is already a member of the same gym silently `UPDATE`d the single `gym_memberships` row's role to the staff role, breaking the member app for that person (`requireRole('member')` 403s) with nothing visible in the admin UI, and a later revoke deleted the row with no way back (`/me/link` requires `clerk_user_id IS NULL`). Intended model written down: staff log in with their work account, membership lives on a personal account. Both sides now refuse with 409: `assertNotMemberEmail` (staff side, runs before `POST /staff` inserts anything, on `PUT` email change, and inside `grantAccess`) and `isStaffLoginEmail` (member side, `POST /members` + `POST /members/:id/invite`), plus `POST /me/link` refuses when the account already holds a non-member row in the gym. No migration, no UI change beyond the error message. New `api/src/test/member-staff-collision.test.ts` (10 cases, both directions incl. case-insensitive match, existing-account member row, pending staff invite, and the `/me/link` guard).)
- **Done (continued)**: #596 (Admin impersonation — role/gym list stayed stale until reload after start/stop: `GymContext` derived the effective role by reading `sessionStorage` during render instead of subscribing to `ImpersonationContext`, and `GymProvider` gets `children` as a stable prop so the parent's `setSession` never re-rendered it; right after starting a `front_desk` session the Staff page still showed **+ New Staff Member** / **Revoke access** until a reload (UI-only — the API always enforced the real role via `x-impersonate-as`). `GymProvider` now consumes `useImpersonation()`; `ImpersonationContext` gained a `ready` flag (mirrors the Member app, #415) with a synchronous storage fallback before rehydration so a mid-session reload never flashes the superadmin role. Frontend-only, no API/migration. Investigated and ruled out in the same ticket: the session does **not** survive *Stop* — storage is cleared synchronously and stays cleared across navigations; the `ERR_ABORTED` next to `/stop → 204` is just how a bodiless 204 is logged.)
- **Done (continued)**: #609 + #610 (Feature flag coverage — found by running the real `filterNavGroups` once per production flag: Professional Services and Assigned Plans were gated on keys never seeded into `feature_flags` (missing key = enabled → never hideable, absent from Cordel → Feature Flags; `/professional-services` also checked the missing key), and Taxes shared `financials.gym_charges` with Products. Migration 160 seeds `organization.professional_services`, `financials.assigned_plans` and `financials.taxes` (copying gym_charges' current value). Taxes menu entry uses `financials.taxes`; `/taxes` reads now depend only on the `financials` group flag (shared tax dropdown in Plans/Products — hiding Products used to break it) and `financials.taxes` gates its five write routes. New `feature-flag-coverage.test.ts`. Permission-matrix mismatch between the admin app and the API split out as #611.)
- **Done (continued)**: #614 (Calendar permission module in the API — #247 made Calendar an independent permission area with front desk read/write, but only the admin app got it; the API still gated `/calendar-events` + `/class-sessions` on TRAINING, where front desk is `R`, so front desk saw create/edit controls that 403'd. Added `CALENDAR` to the API `PERMISSION_MATRIX` (admin/trainers/front desk RW, nutritionist R, accountant/member NONE) and switched both mounts and all 9 write routes to it. Admin app's nutritionist cell corrected RW → R so UI = API. No migration. New `calendar-permissions.test.ts`; `calendar-events.test.ts` front-desk assertion flipped 403 → 201.)
- **Done (continued)**: #613 + #611 (Read-only roles. Part 1/3 (Organization + Calendar pages): new `useModuleAccess(module)` hook (impersonation-aware), `ContextMenuItem.disabled`, `readOnlyStyle`, `common.read_only_hint`. Centers, Spaces, Activity Types (incl. schedule-rule buttons), Professional Services, Operating Hours (weekly grid + holidays), Staff (controls shown disabled instead of hidden; editor Save gated) and Calendar now disable write controls for roles without write access. New `read-only-writes.test.ts` (51 cases across MEMBERS/ORGANIZATION/TRAINING/NUTRITION/FINANCIALS/PAYMENTS). Client redirects stay until #611. Part 2/3 (Financials + Payments, done): Plans (incl. every in-row billing/centers/allowance/price edit button; *Assign to member* follows PAYMENTS, so front desk keeps it), Promotions, Products, Taxes, and the Assigned Plan row (status actions shown disabled instead of hidden; *Close* admin-only via the hook's new `isAdmin`). Transactions, Billing Events and Payment Providers have no write controls. Part 3/3 (Training + Nutrition, this PR): the seven Training/Nutrition pages switch to the hook, show row actions disabled instead of hidden, and gate Add/New/Import-defaults, Clone and Assign-to-member; hook gains `useReadOnlyTitle`.) **#611**: admin `PERMISSION_MATRIX` aligned cell for cell with the API (= #156 spec): trainers + nutritionist gain Organization, nutritionist gains Training, front desk gains Financials (read), accountant loses Members and is read-only on Financials/Payments. The 15 module pages redirect/return-null/load on `canRead` instead of admin/write checks, so read-only roles open them with write controls disabled. New admin tests `permission-matrix-parity.test.ts` (imports the API table) and `nav-by-role.test.ts`; `apps/admin/vitest.config.ts` adds the `@` alias.)
- **Done (continued)**: #607 (Staff logins — Impersonate was empty in every gym but Claude MONEI Test because Staff records created before #592 had no login and saving them never created one, and invited staff looked impersonable but weren't. `PUT /staff/:id` now grants a login to an active record without one (same as create, #594 guard included) and removes it when the record is saved inactive (matches `PATCH /deactivate`); response carries `access`, the form warns an invitation email may be sent. Impersonate dialog lists invited staff greyed out as "Invitation pending"; `POST /platform/impersonation/:targetId` refuses them — reverses the POST half of #358, whose test only passed because the Clerk mock resolves any id (`tenantContext` looks the target up in Clerk on every request). No migration. Tests: 5 new PUT cases in `staff-access.test.ts`, pending-target refusal in `impersonation.test.ts`.)
- **Done (continued)**: #599 (Website self-registration — a gym's WordPress site can now send the member invite email. New `POST /public/gyms/:slug/registrations` (`{ name, email, center_id?, locale? }`), called server-to-server and authenticated by a per-gym API key in `x-api-key`; only a salted scrypt digest is stored, with a prefix pre-filter so junk never reaches scrypt (migration 159: `gyms.website_api_key_hash/_prefix/_created_at` + flag `system.website_integration`). It only creates a Clerk invitation carrying `publicMetadata.gym_signup`; the `members` row is created on first sign-in by the extended `POST /me/link` (201), so fake requests never reach the roster. Always `202` once authenticated (no enumeration; Clerk `422` swallowed = per-email throttle), one shared `401` for bad key/unknown slug, per-IP limiter before DB work + per-gym limiter after the key check (env-tunable). Admin: new **System → Website Integration** page (`GET /system/website-integration`, `POST`/`DELETE …/key` — key shown once, rotate/revoke audited). Also fixes a pre-existing bug: `trust proxy` was never set behind nginx, so every per-IP limiter (incl. the global 500/15 min) was one bucket for the whole platform (`TRUST_PROXY_HOPS`, default 1). `gyms.ts` reads `g.*`, so `stripGymSecrets` keeps the hash out of responses and audit payloads. WordPress setup: `docs/wordpress-integration.md`. Tests: `public-registrations.test.ts`, `website-integration.test.ts`, `me-link-self-registration.test.ts`, unit `website-api-key.test.ts`.)
- **Done (continued)**: #600 (Removed the leftover one-time `POST /dev/seed-gym` route from `api/src/app.ts` — commented "remove after use" and never removed; it sat outside `requireAuth()`. Nothing referenced it: local seeding is `api/src/infra/seed.ts`, gym creation is the superadmin `POST /platform/gyms`. No migration, no UI change. New `api/src/test/dev-seed-gym.test.ts` (3 cases) pins the route as gone and asserts no gym/membership row is written. Convention going forward: no route is mounted in `app.ts` without `requireAuth()` unless it is deliberately public (`/health`, `/docs`, `/public`, `/payment-page`, `/billing`, `/themes`, webhooks).)
- **Done (continued)**: #605 (Docs only — new `docs/go-to-production.md`: single checklist of go-live requirements (GitHub `production` environment, Clerk Production instance, first-superadmin bootstrap via Clerk Dashboard `publicMetadata.platform_role` — not `npm run db:seed`, which also creates a placeholder gym —, public-route audit, Monei/PCI items from `decisions.md`). Linked from `README.md`, `docs/architecture.md` and the `CLAUDE.md` doc-update checklist.)
- **Done (continued)**: #787 (Receipts for recurring payments. `POST /payments/:id/receipt` no longer refuses everything that is not a `payment_recorded`: `isReceiptableEvent()` (`domain/billingEventStatus.ts`, unit-tested) accepts any event that represents money actually received — event type in `payment_recorded`/`recurring_payment`/`failed_billing` **and** derived status `paid` — so the nightly run's settled charges and a `failed_billing` later settled by Retry or Manual payment (#640) both qualify, the latter through the predicate rather than a special case. A `waived_billing`, an `adjustment` and anything failed, expired or pending are refused with a `400` naming the reason. Sequence allocation moved into `issueReceiptNumber()` (`domain/receiptNumbers.ts`), idempotent under `FOR UPDATE`, so one payment can never burn two numbers. `POST /billing/run` now issues automatically for each charge it settles — in a transaction of its own **after** the charge's has committed, failure logged and swallowed, because that transaction records money the provider already took — and reports `receipts_issued`. UI: `can_issue_receipt` on the Billing Event Details response drives an Issue/Download receipt button in Payments → Billing Events (en/es/ca), and the member app's billing history offers the download on any row carrying a number, not only `payment_recorded`. `pdfFetch` moved into `useApiClient` so the two screens share one implementation. No migration. Tests: `receipt-eligibility.unit.test.ts` (15), `receipts.test.ts` (18), plus 4 run cases in `billing-run.test.ts`.)
- **Done (continued)**: #789 (Payment webhook — cleanup could expire a request the member had already paid. `POST /billing/cleanup` expired every `pending` row past `page_token_expires`, which is the deadline for *opening* the checkout link (creation + 10 min), not for paying through it: `GET /payment-page/token/:token` consumes the token as the page loads, and the member then spends minutes in the Card Input and a 3DS redirect, after which Monei may retry its webhook. A row expired inside that window was skipped by the webhook's `pr.status !== 'pending'` guard as already-processed — money taken, no completed request, no `payment_methods` row, no `next_billing_date`, and the member app still offering *Start payment*. Cleanup now expires against **two** deadlines (`domain/paymentRequestExpiry.ts`): a request whose page was **never opened** (`page_token IS NOT NULL` — the page load is the only writer that clears it) still goes with its token, while one that **was** opened keeps the long `PAYMENT_REQUEST_ABANDONED_HOURS` window (default 24, floored at 1) so a terminal webhook can still land on it; `expired` stays the total the workflow parses (#778), with `expired_unopened`/`expired_abandoned` beside it. Belt and braces, as the ticket asked: the webhook also accepts a `completed` payload on an **`expired`** row, because the provider is the source of truth about money — narrow on purpose, so a `failed`/`expired` webhook never revives one and an already-`completed` row is still skipped. §2's coverage gap closed too: `webhooks.test.ts` now asserts the `payment_methods` upsert and its second-payment update, `next_billing_date` as `starts_at` + the assignment's own `ASSIGNMENT_CADENCE` (and left alone when already set), the `billing_event_id` back-link, idempotency under a retried event, and the race end to end through the real page load and the real cleanup; new `me-payment-requests.test.ts` covers `POST /me/payment-requests` for the first time (consent stamp, minor units at the provider boundary, the 409 for two active memberships, the 400 for a cycle that owes nothing, the 3/hour limiter). No migration. New `payment-request-expiry.unit.test.ts`.)
- **Done (continued)**: #821 (Product Billing Frequency is five choices — *Once*, *Per Session*, *4 Weeks*, *Month*, *Year*. **Week** left the dropdown; nothing left the database. `gym_charges_billing_frequency_check` (migration 123) still permits all six values and there is no backfill and no re-mapping, because a weekly item is a real price whose period is neither a calendar month nor 28 days — so "what may be stored" and "what may be configured" became two different answers, held once in `api/src/domain/sellableItemFrequency.ts`. `sellableItemFrequencyWriteError(next, current)` is the one rule both routes apply: `POST /sellable-items` refuses `week` outright, `PUT /sellable-items/:id` accepts it **only** when it is the value the row already stores — which is what keeps a legacy weekly item editable (its form submits the value back untouched) without a 400 and without silently rewriting its frequency — and nothing may be moved onto it. A legacy item keeps classifying as a `periodical` benefit (`isRecurringFrequency()` still counts `week`) and keeps billing; `POST /:id/duplicate` copies it verbatim, since Duplicate is a copy, not the dropdown. Admin: the inline create card and the inline editor build their options from one `frequencyOptions()` (#805), which renders the retired value as a **disabled** option only while the form holds it, under a one-line notice (en/es/ca) saying that choosing another replaces it. No migration. Tests: `sellable-item-frequency.unit.test.ts` (13), six `#821` integration cases in `gym-charges.test.ts` (create refused, each offered frequency created, legacy read/classified/edited/moved-and-not-back, duplicate faithful) and `apps/admin/src/test/sellable-item-frequency.test.ts` (12). Membership Plan billing frequency (#820) is untouched.)
- **Done (continued)**: #820 (Membership Plan Billing Frequency is a single dropdown — *Month* or *4 Weeks*. The Billing & Duration editor's number box plus the whole `recurring_billing_unit` ENUM offered "every 3 days" and "every 2 years", combinations nobody sells; the section now offers the two cadences a gym bills on. **No migration and no new column**: the choice maps onto the same `billing_policies.recurring_billing_interval` + `_unit` pair every assignment snapshots and `advanceBillingDate()` steps, so nothing downstream moves — `api/src/domain/planBillingFrequency.ts` (pure) names the two pairs and `PUT /membership-plans/:id/billing-policy` rejects anything else with a 400, which is what keeps the single dropdown from being a frontend-only rule. A Plan configured before the rule is left alone: `planBillingFrequencyOf()` answers `null` for its cadence, so the card, the Details modal and the editor keep saying "Every 2 months" instead of relabelling it, and the editor warns that saving the section moves it to the selected frequency. Products' own billing frequency is untouched.)
- **Done (continued)**: #823 (A Theme upload control says why it is unavailable. The **Upload logo** and Members App **Upload image** buttons were live even when the gym's Cloudflare bucket could not be written to, so an admin could pick a file, press Save and watch the section come back unchanged — the API's 503 (deployment has no `CLOUDFLARE_R2_*`) and 409 (this gym was never initialized, so there is no `storage_folder_prefix` to build a key from, and none of its folder tree — the gym-level `Themes/` root included, #735) reached them only as a toast, after the fact. Both controls, and their hidden file inputs, are now disabled while either holds, with the reason stated in the section; picking is refused in the page's own handlers too, so nothing can be staged for an upload Save would then attempt. The rule is one pure function — `gymStorageBlock()` (`apps/admin/src/lib/gymStorageReadiness.ts`, `'not_configured' | 'not_initialized' | null`) — and the three controls that had their own inline copy of it (`ImageUploadField`, `ExerciseImageField`, `ExerciseVideoField`) now read it from there, so `activeGym.storage_configured`/`storage_folder_prefix` are named in exactly one place. **Base** Themes are not gated: their logo is a blob on the row and their slots live under `PLATFORM_STORAGE_ROOT` (`cordel/Themes/…`), which no gym's bucket state reaches — the same reason a Base Exercise passes `requiresGymStorage={false}`. Frontend only: no route, no payload and no schema changed, and the logo display, the *Logo contains name of the gym?* option, the accepted formats and the size limits are untouched.)
- **Done (continued)**: #824 (A Theme logo is stored in the **theme's own** Cloudflare folder, and a failed save says what broke. Two defects in one ticket. (1) The key was #713's gym-wide `<storage_folder_prefix>/Branding/Logo/logo.<ext>`, so a gym held one branding logo and every upload handed the slot over — the uploading theme took the key and every sibling dropped its reference. `buildThemeLogoKey()`/`themeLogoFolderKeys()` (`domain/themeLogo.ts`, over the new shared `domain/themeFolders.ts`) make it `<storage_folder_prefix>/Themes/<theme_id>-<sanitized name>/Logo/logo.<ext>` — the sibling of the `Members/` leaf #725 writes — so each theme carries its own logo, the upload deletes only what *its own* row pointed at, the hand-over is gone with the key that required it, and `Branding/` is written by nothing (removing it from Gym Bucket Initialization is #826's). The theme folder and its `Logo/` leaf are created on demand; the gym-level `Themes/` root deliberately is not — it is #735's, so a gym without it stays a 409 and a disabled control (#823). No migration: a row written before this keeps its `Branding/Logo/` key, still renders (the URL is derived from the stored key), and moves the next time its logo is replaced. `infra/storage.ts`' `buildGymLogoKey()`/`BRANDING_LOGO_FOLDER` are deleted. (2) The generic **Unauthorized** the ticket reports was the frontend's: the theme logo and Members background uploads were hand-rolled `fetch('/api/proxy/…')` calls sending only the bearer token, and the Next proxy forwards `x-gym-id` but cannot invent it — so every tenant-scoped upload reached `tenantContext` with no gym. New `uploadFetch()` in `lib/apiClient.ts` sends the same headers `apiFetch` does with the file's own `Content-Type`, and both Themes pages use it. On top of that the storage routes now answer with `stage` (`domain/storageFailureStage.ts` — resolve the path, create the theme folder, create the leaf, upload, remove, save settings) and the `path` they were working on, beside `describeStorageError()`'s existing `details`; `lib/storageErrorMessage.ts`'s pure `formatStorageError()` renders the ticket's Operation / Path / Error / Details block, with the page naming the step only as the fallback for a failure that never reached storage. One locale key per stage in en/es/ca, since next-intl prints a missing key verbatim. Tests: 10 unit cases for the new keys and folder markers in `storage.test.ts`, 7 in `storage-failure-stage.unit.test.ts`, the rewritten `theme-logo-storage.test.ts` (per-theme keys, the folder markers, the legacy `Branding/` row moving on replace, siblings untouched, `stage`/`path` on 409/502/503) and 13 admin cases in `theme-upload-diagnostics.test.ts`.)
- **Done (continued)**: #826 (Gym Bucket Initialization creates exactly three first-level folders. `GYM_FOLDERS` in `api/src/infra/storage.ts` still wrote `Branding/`, `Branding/Logo/`, `Branding/Images/` and `Members/` under every gym root, and nothing has written a byte into any of them since #824 moved the theme logo to `Themes/<theme_id>-<name>/Logo/` and #725 put a theme's six Members App slots under `Themes/<theme_id>-<name>/Members/` — so a freshly initialized bucket showed an admin five empty folders that will never fill. The gym root is now `Nutrition/`, `Exercises/` and `Themes/` and nothing else; the leaves below them (`Nutrition/Images/`, `Exercises/Images/`, `Exercises/Videos/`) are untouched, since the ticket is scoped to what sits directly under the root (§6). The markers keep the spelling the key builders use — `domain/exerciseImages.ts`, `domain/exerciseVideos.ts` and `domain/themeFolders.ts` write `Exercises/Images`, `Exercises/Videos` and `Themes/`, and a marker differing in case alone would add a *fourth* first-level folder beside the one uploads populate rather than rename it; a unit test asserts the two agree. Initialization stays idempotent and write-only, so a gym provisioned earlier keeps the five obsolete markers until someone deletes them — a `docs/go-to-production.md` sweep, which notes that a `themes.logo_object_key` written before #824 may still point into `Branding/Logo/`. No migration, no API change, no frontend change.)
- **Done (continued)**: #827 (A Custom Theme is created only when the gym's Cloudflare bucket is, and creating one initializes the theme's own folders. Cloning was the one way a Custom Theme came into existence and it wrote a `themes` row with no regard for storage, so a gym whose bucket had never been initialized could accumulate themes whose every upload control was disabled (#823) and whose every upload route answered 409 — a theme with nowhere to keep its assets. `POST /system/themes/clone/:sourceId` now resolves the gym's `storage_folder_prefix` **before** the INSERT (503 when the deployment has no `CLOUDFLARE_R2_*`, 409 when this gym was never initialized, each with the `stage`/`path` diagnostic #824 added) and then writes the new theme's three folder markers — `Themes/<theme_id>-<sanitized name>/` with its `Logo/` and `Members/` leaves — through the new `themeStorageFolderKeys()` (`domain/themeFolders.ts`) and `ensureStorageFolders()`. Storage first, row second, which is what makes a partially created theme impossible: a 502 from R2 leaves no row, and the markers are zero-byte keys ending in `/`, so re-writing one is idempotent and can never touch a real object. The clone gets its own folder (the key carries the new id), the gym-level `Themes/` root is still Gym Bucket Initialization's (#735) and is never created here, and a failed marker is named by position — `themeFolderStageForKey()` maps the three keys onto `create_theme_folder`/`create_logo_folder`/`create_members_folder` rather than pattern-matching a key, so a theme *named* "Logo" is not mistaken for its own leaf. The two leaf names moved to `domain/themeFolders.ts` (re-exported by `themeLogo.ts` and `themeMemberImages.ts`) because creation now writes them before either upload feature is involved; their case is load-bearing, since a `logo/` marker would add a folder beside `Logo/` rather than rename it (#826's rule). Admin: `⋮ → Clone` is disabled with the reason as its tooltip while `gymStorageBlock(activeGym)` holds — #823's one rule, no second copy — the submit refuses too, and a failed creation renders the same Operation/Path/Error/Details block every other storage-backed save does. **Base** Themes are untouched: their objects live under `PLATFORM_STORAGE_ROOT` (`cordel/Themes/…`), which no gym's bucket governs. No migration, no schema change. Tests: 18 unit cases (`theme-storage-folders.unit.test.ts`, `storage-failure-stage.unit.test.ts`), the new `theme-storage-init.test.ts` (409/503 create nothing, the three markers, the roots untouched, the clone's own path, 502 per leaf, plus the clone cases moved out of `gym-themes.test.ts`, which mocks no R2 client) and 8 admin cases in `theme-create-storage-gating.test.ts`.)
- **Done (continued)**: #828 (**Initialize bucket** on both Theme context menus — the manual, repeatable counterpart to the initialization #827 does at creation time. A Theme created before #827, or one renamed since (a rename moves its folder and nothing re-creates it until the next upload), had no way to get its folder tree back short of uploading something. Two routes, one per storage root: `POST /system/themes/:id/storage/initialize` (gym admin, the Theme's folder under the gym's `storage_folder_prefix`) and `POST /platform/themes/:id/storage/initialize` (superadmin, under `PLATFORM_STORAGE_ROOT`), each answering only for its own rows — a Base Theme is 404 on the gym route and a Custom Theme 404 on the platform one, so neither reaches the other's objects by changing the Theme id. Both write exactly `themeStorageFolderKeys()` (#827): the theme's own folder plus its `Logo/` and `Members/` leaves, zero-byte markers whose keys end in `/`, so a re-run ensures the structure and can never delete or overwrite an object — and neither route touches the `themes` row, so the Theme's id, name and configuration are untouched (§3). The gym root and its `Themes/` branch stay Gym Bucket Initialization's (#735): a gym with no prefix is a 409 rather than having its tree papered over, which is §5's distinction, and the gym-side menu item is disabled with the reason through `gymStorageBlock()` exactly as Clone is (#823). The platform side is gated by no gym's bucket, so only the deployment-wide 503 applies there. Failures report the marker that broke, reusing #824's `stage`/`path`/`details` — new `formatStorageErrorLine()` in `lib/storageErrorMessage.ts` renders the same diagnostic on one line, because `Toast` renders its message as a single text node. The clone route's inline folder-writing block became `ensureThemeStorage()` in `gym-themes.ts`, shared with the new route so the keys, the stage and the 502 shape have one definition. No migration, no schema change. Tests: 21 integration cases in `theme-bucket-initialize.test.ts` (idempotence, markers only, the roots untouched, the row untouched, cross-router 404s, 401/403, 409/503/502 per leaf) and 14 admin cases in `theme-bucket-initialize.test.ts`.)
- **Done (continued)**: #830 (A failed Theme asset upload says what broke, and one asset failing no longer takes the others with it. Three defects. (1) The **broken `logo preview`** the ticket is filed with was the Next proxy, not the Theme editor: `app/api/proxy/[...path]/route.ts` read every backend response with `res.text()`, and a UTF-8 decode of binary replaces each invalid byte with U+FFFD. A Base Theme's logo is a `logo_bytes` blob served as raw bytes by `GET /themes/:id/logo` (it has no R2 `logo_url`), so the `<img>` received a corrupted PNG carrying the right `Content-Type` — nothing errored, the browser simply could not decode it. Both proxies now forward `await res.arrayBuffer()`, byte-exact for JSON too; the Admin one also carries the receipt PDFs `pdfFetch()` reads with `res.blob()`, which had the same corruption. (2) Each Theme screen carried **its own copy** of the Save sequence, and the copies had drifted: the Base Themes page removed a logo and a Members slot through a bare `apiFetch` with no diagnostic, so a failure there was the `Unauthorized` #824 existed to replace, and its settings `PUT` had none either. The sequence is now declared once in `apps/admin/src/components/themes/themeAssetSave.ts` — `planThemeAssetOps()` (the logo first, then the six slots in editor order, a picked file always beating a queued removal), `themeAssetOpRequest()` (the per-asset path under the router root the page passes in, `/system/themes` or `/platform/themes`, so #806's rule holds and no permission decision moves into shared code), `runThemeAssetOps()` and the failure formatting. (3) The first failure **aborted every asset after it**: the loop threw, so a rejected `training` upload meant `nutrition`, `calendar` and the rest were never attempted. `runThemeAssetOps()` attempts all of them and throws nothing, the page renders every failure as its own Operation/Path/Error/Details block (`formatThemeAssetFailures()`), and `pendingAfterFailures()` keeps exactly the failed assets queued with their files and previews, so pressing Save again retries those and re-uploads none of the successes. A Members failure names its slot (`storage_error_title_members_image_slot`, `{slot}` interpolated), each failing control is marked next to itself (`logoError`, `slotErrors`), an error with nothing usable in it gets `storage_error_fallback` instead of an empty `Error:` line, and a preview whose URL will not load renders `logo_preview_unavailable` rather than the browser's broken-image icon. The only step that still aborts the Save is the settings `PUT`: the asset keys are built from the theme's persisted name, so there is nothing to gain from uploading against a rename that did not happen. Storage locations, validation rules, Remove semantics and the successful path are unchanged (§Existing Successful Behavior, §Remove Behavior). No migration, no API change. Tests: 37 admin cases in `theme-upload-error-handling.test.ts` (the plan and its ordering, the per-asset paths under both roots, every asset attempted despite a failure, the API error carried through, the diagnostic and its fallback, what stays queued, both proxies reading bytes, both pages on the shared module, and every interpolated key present in `gym_themes` and `themes` across en/es/ca); `theme-members-images.test.ts` and `base-theme-members-images.test.ts` follow the sequence to its new home.)
- **Done (continued)**: #832 (**Mandatory** on Products. A new `gym_charges.mandatory` flag (migration 200, `tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`), rendered as a single checkbox in the inline create card and the inline editor and as a Yes/No row on the read-only expanded card and in the Details modal. One column and one control serve both kinds of row the page holds — the per-gym System items seeded from `charge_types` (`is_system=1`, the ticket's "Base Products", the Insurance Fee among them) and the gym's own (`is_system=0`, "Custom") — because both live in this one table, so there are no two implementations to keep consistent. The flag is **editable on a System item**, which is why `PUT /sellable-items/:id` writes it outside the `IF(? = 0, …)` `is_system` guard that still freezes a System row's name/type/units, and as `mandatory = IF(?, ?, mandatory)` keyed on whether the request supplied the field (the shape `PUT /promotions/:id` uses for `only_applicable_for_new_members`) — a `COALESCE` on the value itself could never persist an unchecked box. `validateMandatory()` accepts a boolean or 0/1 and 400s on anything else, so the truthy string `"false"` cannot silently mark an item mandatory. `POST /sellable-items` defaults it to `0`, existing items are non-mandatory by the column default (no backfill statement), and `POST /:id/duplicate` copies it verbatim. No CHECK: the router coerces to `? 1 : 0` and adding one would rebuild `gym_charges` under `ALGORITHM=COPY`. **Nothing reads the flag** — automatic inclusion in Billing Plans, preventing removal from one, Billing Plan pricing, Membership Plan Benefits and any Insurance Fee special case are explicitly out of scope and belong to the later ticket that consumes the attribute. Tests: eleven `#832` integration cases in `gym-charges.test.ts` (default, create both ways, read-back on `GET /:id` and the list, PUT toggling both ways, PUT leaving it alone, editable on a System item whose name/type still are not, 0/1 accepted and a non-boolean 400d, an explicit null treated as absent, duplicate) and nine admin cases in `sellable-items-mandatory.test.ts`.)
- **Done (continued)**: #833 (Members App theme settings with per-setting Admin inheritance. The shared Theme editor gains a **Members App** section holding five collapsible sub-sections — `Header (Members App)`, `Background (Members App)`, `Section Cards (Members App)`, `Text (Members App)`, `Calendar (Members App)` — and the 18 settings of §8's mapping. Both Theme screens render one component (`ThemeMembersAppEditor`) over one declaration (`apps/admin/src/lib/membersAppTokens.ts`), so Base Themes and Custom Themes get the same settings, sources and UI with no second editor (§15). **No migration and no schema change**: `tokens.membersApp` is an overrides-only map, so an inherited setting is never stored (§13, §19) and `Restore inherited value` deletes the key — dropping the map when it empties — rather than writing the Admin value into it. That is what keeps inheritance dynamic: an Admin colour edited later moves every setting still inherited from it and none that was overridden, decided per setting (§12, §18). Each control carries the `Inherited`/`Custom` badge the `advanced` attributes already use plus a secondary line naming its source — `inherited from Header Background` — and `validateMembersApp()` (`api/src/domain/membersAppTokens.ts`, reached through `validateTokens()`, so both routers apply it) refuses an unknown key, a non-hex colour, a font outside `FONT_STACKS`, a separator width outside 0–20 and a blank CSS length; `HEX_RE`/`FONT_STACKS` moved to `domain/themeTokenFormats.ts` so the two validators need not import each other. Two Admin sources genuinely did not exist and were added as `advanced` attributes beside the settings they belong to, each wired to a CSS variable a real surface reads — `headerTextFont` → `--gd-header-font` (`TopHeader`) and `cardBorderWidth` → `--gd-card-border-width` (`cardSurfaceStyle`) — because an attribute the editor persists that nothing reads is the #677 defect. The two §14 flagged as possibly missing were **not** added: `Application Surface` is the existing `advanced.modalBackground` and `Input Background` the existing `colors.inputBackgroundColor`, reused per §7/§14 rather than duplicated, and the modal settings deliberately never read `calendarBackground`. On the Members App side `ThemeProvider` calls `applyMembersAppTokens()` after `applyTokens()`, so a setting sharing a variable with its source (`--gd-app-bg`, `--gd-calendar-*`, `--gd-color-h1..h3`, `--gd-header-sep-*`) wins, and the rest get `--gd-members-*` variables read by `TopBar` (which had been borrowing the Admin **sidebar** colour for the header), `MembersSectionCard` (the border, on the one component every navigation card renders through) and the member Calendar page (the event window and its dropdowns); the member pages' heading colours now read `--gd-color-h1..h3` with their current values as the fallback, so Title 1/2/3 reach a surface. No Members App preview (§16), and the editor holds no save state — it hands the page a token draft, so the existing Save/Cancel and dirty state cover it (§17). Tests: 24 admin cases in `members-app-theme-settings.test.ts` (the mapping against §8 transcribed from the ticket, every source resolving to a setting that exists, override/restore/null semantics, the editor's structure, the locale keys in en/es/ca across both namespaces, and the two mirrors), 16 member cases in `members-app-theme-vars.test.ts` (mirror agreement, resolution, overriding a shared variable, no setting left unpainted), 11 API unit cases in `members-app-tokens.unit.test.ts`, plus integration cases on both Theme routers.)
- **Done (continued)**: #809 (Nutrition → Dashboard — Nutrition Plan overview cards. The `Nutrition → Dashboard` page (`apps/admin/src/app/[locale]/nutrition/page.tsx`, a placeholder until now) renders one card per **Nutrition Plan Template** — its name, the number of **active members** holding a Nutrition Plan created from it, and the Template's own status badge — plus one bucket card, *No base nutrition template plan*, for the plans created from scratch (`member_nutrition_plans.template_id IS NULL`), which the issue owner asked for by name on the thread. `GET /nutrition/dashboard/nutrition-plans` (`api/src/api/nutrition-dashboard.ts`, new router mounted on the **`nutrition` group flag** so switching the Nutrition Plans page off leaves the Dashboard readable — #638's Finance Dashboard choice) answers it in one aggregate query, driven by the assignments rather than by the Templates: a card therefore exists only where a counted member exists (no empty `0` cards, §4), the Template's status is never the filter (an `inactive` or `draft` Template shows as long as an active member is on it, §7) and a plan whose Template row belongs to another gym resolves to no Template and falls into the bucket, so no other gym's Template name can be read off the endpoint. A Template soft-deleted while members still hold plans from it keeps its card with `deleted` as the status — dropping it would silently remove those members from the overview. **Active member** is the Members page's `enrollment_status = 'active'` (the member's latest `user_memberships` row) plus `members.deleted_at IS NULL`, and that derivation now lives once in `api/src/domain/memberEnrollment.ts` (`latestEnrollmentStatusSql()`), read by both `GET /members` and this endpoint — a gym owner reading `12 active members` off a card and then filtering the Members page by `Enrollment status: Active` gets the same set. Only `active` Nutrition Plans count (a `completed` one is no longer currently assigned), and the count is of **members**, so a member holding two plans from the same Template counts once. No migration, no write path, no change to Nutrition Plan creation, editing, assignment or Template behaviour. Tests: `api/src/test/nutrition-dashboard.test.ts` (auth, the two feature flags, tenant isolation, which cards show, the bucket and its ordering, and the counting rules) and `api/src/test/member-enrollment.unit.test.ts`)
- **Done (continued)**: #818 (Membership Plan **Example Timeline** — the Plan card's simulation is now the Promotion card's. The #485 *Billing Events Forecast* listed the next ten charges from price × cadence and never looked at Billing & Duration, so a Plan with a two-month Free Period forecast a charge for each of those months; the section is replaced (same slot, `PLAN_SECTION_ORDER`'s `section_example_timeline`) by a Period / Dates / Status / Billing table, **one row per billing period of the Plan's own cadence**. `api/src/domain/planExampleTimeline.ts` (pure) is the projection: rows step with `advanceBillingDate()` over the stored `billing_policies` pair — a legacy cadence included, stepped exactly as stored — and each row's Status is `classifyPlanDurationPeriod()` evaluated at its own start date, i.e. the one rule the nightly run, `resolveMembershipFee()` and `GET /me/membership` already price a cycle with. That is the thread's Q1 answer, "only the simulation": the durations stay calendar months, so a 4-weekly Plan with Free Period = 2 shows **three** free rows rather than advertising a charge the run does not make. Rows = Free + Paid + Bonus + 2 trailing regular ones (Q5), capped at `MAX_TIMELINE_PERIODS`; the last row is open-ended only when it is regular. The Billing column quotes the Plan's current price **VAT-inclusive** as the server computed it (`amount_incl_tax`, gross alone for a gym with no rate — no arithmetic in the frontend, #817) and `No charge` for a Free / Bonus / Pre-paid period; a Plan with no price reads `—`, never €0.00. Statuses read *Free (benefit)* / *Prepaid (benefit)* / *Pay (benefit)* / *Bonus (benefit)* / *Pay (regular)* (Q3) — the Plan's vocabulary, never the Promotion's. `enrichPlan` embeds it as `example_timeline` and `GET /membership-plans/:id/example-timeline` replaces `…/billing-forecast`; `domain/billingForecast.ts` and its unit test are deleted. No migration, nothing persisted, no billing rule changed — Auto-renew and assignment stayed out of scope per the thread. The table itself is the shared `apps/admin/src/components/ExampleTimeline.tsx`, rendered by **both** the Plan and Promotion cards (the ticket's "do not introduce a second simulation design"), presentational only: labels, classification and the Billing cell stay with each page. Tests: `plan-example-timeline.test.ts` (13 unit), reshaped `#818` cases in `membership-plans.test.ts`, `charge-benefits-retired.test.ts`, `membership-plan-benefits.test.ts` and `apps/admin/src/test/plan-example-timeline.test.ts` (13).)
- **Done (continued)**: #829 (Theme assets land in the folders the ticket names, and a Base Theme logo is an object like every other asset. Two halves. (1) **The spelling.** The theme tree was `Themes/<theme_id>-<name>/` with `Logo/` and `Members/` leaves; it is now `themes/<theme_id>-<name>/` with `logo/` and `members_app/`, which also lowercases the gym root's third first-level folder (`THEMES_FOLDER` in `api/src/infra/storage.ts`, `THEME_LOGO_FOLDER`/`THEME_MEMBERS_FOLDER` in `domain/themeFolders.ts` — one constant each, so every key builder, folder marker and initialization route moved together). R2 has no directories, so nothing was renamed and nothing was rewritten: a row's stored key is the only way back to its object, so assets already in the bucket keep rendering under the old names and move on their next upload — the owner sweeps the old tree by hand, which is the thread's answer ("I'll delete the old structure manually so no migration is needed"), and `docs/go-to-production.md` carries the item. (2) **The Base Theme logo.** `POST /platform/themes/:id/logo` wrote a `themes.logo_bytes` MEDIUMBLOB, the one asset still in the database, because #824 had no platform folder to upload into; `cordel/` has been a real root since #732, so it now stores `cordel/themes/<theme_id>-<sanitized name>/logo/logo.<ext>` and keeps the key in `themes.logo_object_key` with `logo_bytes` cleared — the Custom Theme's own columns, not a second store (the thread's "No logo_bytes. Just reuse the same fields"), and the URL stays derived (`themeLogoUrl()`), so `shapeTheme()` on that router returns `logo_url` too. Same 512 KB / PNG-SVG-JPEG-WebP validation, extension from the validated MIME (the uploaded file name reaches no key), folders created on demand (`themeRootFolderKeys()` + `themeLogoFolderKeys()` — `cordel/` and `cordel/themes/` included, since no Gym Bucket Initialization owns the platform root), best-effort sweep of the key it replaced, `DELETE` removing the object first and reporting a 502 rather than dropping the reference, and `stage`/`path` diagnostics throughout — minus the gym route's 409, which a constant root cannot have. 404 for a gym's Custom Theme, as the gym route is 404 for a Base Theme (§8). `GET /themes/:id/logo` is untouched and still serves either mode, so a logo uploaded before this keeps rendering from its blob. No migration and no schema change. Tests: `base-theme-logo-storage.test.ts` (25, the new platform route end to end) and `theme-asset-paths.unit.test.ts` (8, the ticket's §1–§4 tree pinned for both roots — logo plus all six slots), plus the renamed paths across the nine existing theme-storage suites and the admin/member source guards.)

- **Done (continued)**: #882 (Members **PROFILE** reads in the layout it is edited in — a presentation-only change to the expanded Member row. Both halves now render the same `MemberProfileLayout` (`apps/admin/src/app/[locale]/members/MemberProfileLayout.tsx`): one grid, one field order, one set of labels, Notes full-width and Assigned Centers / Default Center in one position, at the same breakpoints. Each half still owns only the contents of a cell — an `<input>`/`<select>`/`<textarea>` on one side, the persisted value on the other — and the read-only side carries no control, no help text and no Edit affordance (#797 unchanged). The declaration in `memberProfile.ts` grew each field's `editLabelKey`, `placeholderKey` and `helpKey`, so the Edit form no longer hand-writes an input per field and Name keeps its required marker in the form alone. - **Done (continued)**: #883 (the **mobile navigation drawer scrolls**. The drawer was bounded to the viewport below the top bar, but the panel inside it carried `min-height: 100vh` and nothing was allowed to overflow, so with several groups expanded the lower navigation items sat past the bottom of the screen and no swipe could reach them. The wrapper now bounds (`100dvh` with the `100vh` fallback, `overflow: hidden`), the panel fills it (`flex: 1; min-height: 0`, no viewport height of its own) and the `<nav>` is the single scroll container, with `overscroll-behavior: contain` and `-webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch`; the page behind is frozen by a `sidebar-drawer-open` class on `<body>` scoped to the mobile media query. The nav became a block container rather than a column flex box, so an expanded group overflows instead of squeezing its neighbours. Navigation items, hierarchy, permissions, routes, active-route detection, expand/collapse and the drawer's own open/close behaviour are untouched, and the desktop sidebar takes no height or overflow rule. Frontend only: no migration, no API change, no new locale key. Tests: `apps/admin/src/test/mobile-sidebar-scroll.test.ts` (10).)
- **Done (continued)**: #884 (Icons on the sidebar's first-level navigation. Each of the nine collapsible groups now carries an icon beside its expand/collapse chevron — Membership (people), Calendar, Organization (building), Training (dumbbell), Nutrition (apple), Payments (card), Financials (banknote), Configuration (sliders) and Cordel (shield). The icon is **named in the nav config** (`icon: NavIconName`, required, so a group added later cannot reach the sidebar without one) and drawn by one registry, `apps/admin/src/components/icons/NavIcons.tsx`; `NavGroup` renders `<NavIcon name={group.icon} />` and branches on nothing. The nine are one set, not nine drawings: a single `IconShell` declares the 24×24 grid, the 1.75 stroke, the round joins and the 16px box, they stroke in `currentColor` and fill nothing — so they follow the header's existing colour rather than adding a state — and each `<svg>` is `aria-hidden`/`focusable="false"`, leaving the label as the accessible name. No icon library was added: nine icons do not pay for a dependency, and the components follow the app's inline-style shape. The chevron is untouched and keeps its own job; the only spacing that moved is the group header's padding (20px → 16px) and gap (8px → 6px), which is what buys the icon its room — measured across en/es/ca, the longest label (`ENTRENAMIENTO`, 196px) still fits the 220px sidebar on one line, and every label starts at the same x since neither the chevron nor the `<svg>` may shrink. Second-level items stay text-only. Frontend only: no migration, no API change, no locale key, no route, role, feature-key gate, hierarchy or expand/collapse behaviour touched. Tests: `apps/admin/src/test/nav-icons.test.ts` (13).)
- **Done (continued)**: #893 (**Mandatory Products are always part of a Membership Plan.** #832 added `gym_charges.mandatory` and deliberately left it unread; this is the ticket that consumes it, for the Membership Plan Benefit sections and nothing else (§9 scopes out Promotion benefits, billing logic and Product pricing). The rule lives once, in `api/src/domain/mandatoryPlanBenefits.ts` (pure): `mandatoryItemsForCategory()` picks the gym's **active, non-deleted** mandatory items that classify into one section through `classifySellableItem()` (#550, never a second mapping), `mergeMandatoryBenefits()` reports the ones a Plan has no stored row for as part of the section (`implicit: true`, `quantity: 1` — §5's "appropriate default configuration"), and `withMandatoryBenefits()` is what a replace-all `PUT` writes. Two decisions: **the server decides, not the editor** — §7 is explicit that the frontend must not be able to bypass the rule, so `GET /membership-plans/:id/{session,oneoff,periodical}-benefits` and `enrichPlan`'s three embedded sections merge, and the `PUT` writes a mandatory item whether or not the client sent it; and **a `PUT` missing one preserves it rather than 400s** (§7 allows either), which is what makes §5 work on its own — an existing Plan picks up a newly mandatory item the first time any of its sections is saved. A submitted mandatory item passes through untouched, quantity included (§4: mandatory says the item must exist, never what its quantity is), and §8's no-duplicates rule is the merge's own `has` check. An **inactive or soft-deleted** mandatory item is ignored: the benefit `PUT` already refuses a newly selected inactive item, and forcing one in would fail every Plan save on a catalogue change nobody asked for. Frontend: the shared `SellableItemBenefits.tsx` gains an explicit `enforceMandatory` opt-in the Plans page passes to both halves of each section — a mandatory row renders its item as a labelled value with a `Mandatory` pill instead of a picker and has no `✕` control, so the item can be neither dropped nor swapped away, and the form (never the read-only view, #797) carries the sentence that says why. Promotions pass nothing and are unchanged. The benefit `SELECT` joins `gc.mandatory` as `gym_charge_mandatory`; no migration, no new endpoint, and nothing in billing or the Assigned Plan snapshot changes — a mandatory item reaches an assignment's snapshot the ordinary way, as a stored Plan benefit row. Tests: `mandatory-plan-benefits.unit.test.ts` (13 pure cases) plus thirteen `#893` integration cases in `membership-plan-benefits.test.ts` and twelve admin cases in `plan-mandatory-benefits.test.ts`.)
- **Done (continued)**: #892 (**Membership Plan durations are counted in the Plan's Billing Frequency periods.** A Plan billed every 4 Weeks showed `Paid Duration: 2 month(s)`, and meant it: the Example timeline stepped rows by the cadence while `classifyPlanDurationPeriod()` stepped statuses by `advanceBillingDate(startsAt, n, 'month')` with the unit hard-coded, so the rows and their statuses were measured differently — #818's Q1 answer, which this ticket reverses in the nightly run as well as in the preview. The four durations are now counts of the Plan's own Billing Frequency periods: `PlanDuration` carries the cadence it is counted in and `toPlanDuration()` takes it as a **required** argument (the reason `personalFeeBenefit` is required, #772), resolved through `ASSIGNMENT_CADENCE` for an assignment and `billing_policies` for a Plan, falling back to `1 month` — what every duration meant before — when there is none. There is still exactly one classifier, and `computePlanExampleTimeline()` re-binds the duration to the cadence it steps, so a 4-weekly Plan with Free Period = 2 shows exactly **two** free rows. Migration 201 renames the columns the owner asked for on `membership_plans` **and** on every assignment's frozen snapshot (`free_periods` / `paid_periods` / `pay_beforehand_periods` / `bonus_periods`), dropping and re-adding migration 189's CHECK around the rename; `RENAME COLUMN` is in-place in MySQL 8, no value is converted and no data is reinterpreted at rest (§6/§7 — changing the frequency changes the unit, never the numbers), and a Plan on the Month cadence bills exactly as it did. `FEE_ASSIGNMENT_COLUMNS` gained the cadence pair and `FEE_ASSIGNMENT_FROM` a LEFT JOIN on `billing_policies`, so every path that prices a fee — the nightly run, the Billing Simulation, `GET /me/membership`, the Payments dashboard, the Promotion apply/revoke adjustment — reads the same unit. Admin: each number box in the Billing & Duration editor is suffixed with the **selected** frequency (`× 4 Weeks`, so the dropdown changes the unit and never the values), and the card summary, the Details modal and the Assigned Plan snapshot render through one `formatPlanDurationPeriods()` — `2 × 4 Weeks`, `2 month(s)` for a monthly Plan, `2 period(s)` for a pre-#820 cadence or a Plan with no billing policy. **Promotions are untouched**, per the ticket's closing note: `promotions.free_months`/`paid_months`/`bonus_months` and `promotionTimeline.ts` keep their calendar months, so a Plan period and a Promotion month can appear in the same simulation. `docs/go-to-production.md` gains the deploy-order item (migrate before deploying the API) and the announcement that a 4-weekly Plan's free and bonus windows get shorter, which raises real charges. Tests: `plan-duration.test.ts` and `plan-example-timeline.test.ts` extended with Month / 4 Weeks / legacy-cadence cases asserting the period **dates**, a new integration file `plan-duration-billing-frequency.test.ts` (the nightly run charging the third 4-week cycle a calendar-month reading would have waived, the timeline, the editor's payload, and Promotions staying on months), and `apps/admin/src/test/plan-duration-periods.test.ts` (20).)
- **Done (continued)**: #894 (**Mandatory shows in the Product card header.** #832 gave `gym_charges.mandatory` a checkbox in both forms and a row in the two read-only surfaces, so learning whether an item was mandatory meant expanding the card or opening its Edit form. The collapsed header's name cell now carries a compact `Mandatory` pill beside the existing `System` one, in that order, for an item whose `mandatory` column is set — read from the column and never from the item's name or type (§4), so it works identically for a per-gym System item (the Insurance Fee) and a Custom one. The badge is read-only (§5): no checkbox, no handler, and the Status and Enrollment `StatusBadge` columns, the two forms' checkboxes and the payloads are untouched. The pill look moved into `listChrome.ts` as `listNameBadgeStyle` rather than being restated a fourth time — the three pages that draw a name-cell badge (Products, Taxes, Professional Services) now read it from there, which is the #724 rule applied to this one piece of chrome. Frontend only: no migration, no API change (`GET /sellable-items` already projects `gc.mandatory`), one new locale key per language (`sellable_items.mandatory_badge`, kept separate from the form's `label_mandatory`).
