- **In progress**: #949 stage 1 of 3 (**Sellable Item is called Product.** The thing a gym sells was named *Sellable Item* on screen while its table has been `gym_charges` since migration 102, its audit entity type `gym_charge` and its feature flag `financials.gym_charges` — so the ticket's §5 ("`sellable_items` → `products`") named a table that never existed, and the rename had to be measured before it could be planned: 2,179 occurrences of `sellable` across 174 files, 28 of them file names, beside 1,229 of `gym_charge`. The thread's `Q1 C` answer takes the rename all the way down to the schema, `Q2` rules out keeping `/sellable-items` as a compatibility alias, and `Q3` scopes *Product Type* to `gym_charges.type` alone, leaving the global `charge_types` lookup as *Charge Type*. This stage is the half a gym owner reads and nothing else: the 16 locale keys per language whose **value** named the entity — the nav label, the page title, the empty state, `+ Add Product`, *Product Details*, the delete confirmation, the retired-frequency notice, the *Product* column of the Membership Plan, Promotion and Assigned Plan benefit grids, the Additional Products column, the Mandatory-benefit hint, the Promotion requirement hint, the Promotion simulation's empty state and the Tax Rate impact list — in en/es/ca, using the translations the app had already settled on (*Productos* / *Productes*, from #957's ADDITIONAL PRODUCTS section, whose own item column still read *Sellable Item* beside the heading that did not). All four `docs/*.md` and `CLAUDE.md` move with it, in **prose only**: a backtick-delimited span is left exactly as it is, so every file path, type name, route and SQL identifier the docs quote still names what is really there — `api/src/domain/sellableItemFrequency.ts` is still that file until stage 2 and `PUT /sellable-items/:id` still that route until stage 3 — and `CLAUDE.md` gains the bullet that says so. One prose occurrence stays on purpose: `staff-sellable` in `architecture.md` is the adjective, not the entity. No identifier, locale key, route, column or migration is touched, so there is no behaviour to regress; the one new file is `api/src/test/product-terminology.unit.test.ts` (10 cases), which holds the line by scanning every locale value of both apps in all three languages for the retired English word and the retired Spanish and Catalan phrasings, while deliberately judging no key — `col_sellable_item` is correct until stage 2 renames it — and bare *artículo* / *article* stays allowed, since it is ordinary Spanish and Catalan for an item of any kind. It sits in the API suite because CI runs `npm test` in `api/` only. Stage 2 is the identifiers, the 28 file names, the locale keys and the admin route folder; stage 3 is the `/products` API root and the `Q1 C` database tier — `RENAME TABLE gym_charges TO products`, `gym_charge_id` across twelve tables, the eight `gym_charges_*` CHECKs, the feature-flag key and the `audit_logs.entity_type` backfill — and carries the `Closes`. API unit suites green at 713; `tsc --noEmit` clean on `api/` and `apps/admin`.)

- **In progress**: #956 stage 1 of 2 (**One Member, one Membership Plan — the backend invariant.** This reverses #634 §6/§14, which had widened the rule to "several plans in parallel, but only one of each type" (migration 172); the owner confirmed the reversal and every open question on the thread (Q1–Q5). Migration **213** restores migration 007's `user_memberships_one_active` UNIQUE index on `(active_member_key)` alone, and first **sweeps** the Members who own more than one live row — status-first (`active` before `paused`), then latest `starts_at`, then latest `id` survives; the rest are cancelled, dated like a replacement and given a `status_changed` ledger row carrying a `Migration 213 (#956): superseded by assignment #N` marker, the only thing that tells a swept row from one an admin closed. The index forbids two *active* rows, so those violators had to go one way or the other; closing a Member down to one *live* row is #956 Q2's product decision on top of that, not an index prerequisite. `ADD` comes before `DROP` and both assert `ALGORITHM=INPLACE, LOCK=NONE`, so a failed swap leaves the old constraint standing instead of none, and a server that could only do it by rebuilding the table says so; the swept row's `ends_at` is clamped with `GREATEST(…, starts_at)`, because the keeper is picked status-first and can have started before a newer `paused` row; and the migration **refuses to run** while any live assignment carries no `gym_id`, naming the ids, since `billing_events.gym_id` is NOT NULL. `npm run memberships:multi-active` (`scripts/report-multi-active-memberships.ts`) is the read-only report that identifies them *before* the migration runs, in two halves: the **owned** conflicts the sweep cancels (naming the keeper in advance) and the **covered** ones it deliberately leaves, where a Member is covered by two live plans but owns neither — cancelling a family plan because one of the people it covers has their own would take it away from everyone else on it, so that one is the gym's call. The rules are declared once in `domain/oneActivePlan.ts` (`LIVE_ASSIGNMENT_STATUSES` = `active` + `paused`, per Q2 — otherwise pausing would be a way around the rule, and a future-dated row is stored `active` so it counts too; `activePlanConflictBody()`; `supersedeStartsAtError()`) and the SQL half in `api/one-active-plan.ts` (`findLiveAssignmentsForMembers()` — `FOR UPDATE`, matching through `user_membership_members` so a **covered member of someone else's family plan already has a plan**, per Q4 — and `supersedeLiveAssignments()`). All three creation paths call them **inside their own insert transaction**: `POST /user-memberships`, `POST /user-memberships/:id/assign-new-plan` and `POST /membership-plans/:id/assign`. An unconfirmed caller gets **`409 { error: 'active_plan_exists', message, current_plan, conflicts }`** carrying both plan names and the current plan's dates, so stage 2's dialog needs no second read; `confirm: true` proceeds and the superseded row becomes **`cancelled`** with `closed_at` stamped now and `ends_at` set to the new plan's `starts_at` (Q3 — immediate, not scheduled; it was `expired` with no date at all before), so the two plans meet at one date and never overlap. A `starts_at` earlier than the plan being replaced is a 400. Assign New Plan needs no `confirm` (replacement is its contract) but does for a *second* live assignment it was not asked about; the Plans page's Assign is all-or-nothing across the selected Members. `POST /user-memberships/:id/members` is the fourth path the rule reaches and the only one with no `confirm` — covering a Member who already has a plan is a 409 naming it, since a replacement needs a new `starts_at` to end the old plan on and coverage has none. Concurrency is covered twice: two assignments of a Member who already has a plan serialise on its row lock, two for a Member who has none collide on the index. Knock-on: `POST /me/payment-requests`'s `409 multiple_active_memberships` is **removed** (the owner: "Remove, no need to preserve anything") — `user_membership_id` is still accepted and still narrows the read; the consolidated Member Billing Simulation is unchanged, as agreed. The four admin call sites now surface `body.message` through a new `apiErrorMessage()` so the interim error line reads a sentence rather than `active_plan_exists`. New `one-active-plan.unit.test.ts` (11), `one-active-membership-plan.test.ts` (19 — every path, both Q2 statuses, the covered-member case, cancel-the-dialog, the dates, the ledger row, tenant isolation and both concurrency shapes) and `one-active-plan-migration.test.ts` (9 — the sweep against the real migration, plus the report); the existing tests that asserted #634's parallel plans are inverted or rewritten against the shape #956 produces. **Stage 2** is the confirmation dialog, built from this 409.)

- **#554 (done)**: Suitable Membership Plans section for Promotions. The
  association (`promotion_membership_plans`, migration 020), its
  `GET`/`PUT /promotions/:id/plans` endpoints, and the apply-time eligibility
  check (`applyPromotionToMembership`/`POST /user-memberships/:id/promotions`
  in `membership-promotions.ts`, both already rejecting a promotion that
  doesn't target the membership's plan) all predate this ticket (#26, #511) —
  no new table or enforcement plumbing was needed. This ticket: (1) tightens
  `PUT /plans` server-side validation — a *newly added* plan id must belong
  to the gym and be active (`lifecycle_status='active'`, `deleted_at IS
  NULL`); an id the promotion was *already* associated with is let through
  even if that plan has since gone inactive, so an unrelated re-save of the
  promotion never drops a pre-existing association (Historical Integrity);
  duplicate ids in the request are deduped instead of hitting the table's
  unique constraint; (2) fixes a frontend bug where the read-only view mode
  resolved an associated plan's name against the *active-only* plans list,
  so an inactive-but-associated plan rendered as `#<id>`, by rendering
  directly off `GET /plans`' own `{id, name}` rows instead; (3) renames the
  "Applicable Plans" section to "Suitable Membership Plans" and adds a
  Select All checkbox (checked/unchecked/indeterminate, only ever acting on
  the *displayed* active plans — pure logic in
  `apps/admin/src/lib/suitablePlansSelection.ts`, unit tested), dedicated
  loading/empty/error states for the active-plans fetch (split out of the
  page's other, best-effort lookups), and secondary info (current price or
  enrollment status) next to each plan name. `promotions.test.ts` gains a
  "Suitable Membership Plans" describe block (active-only new selections,
  cross-tenant rejection, dedup, removal, historical-preservation,
  role/tenant guards); no migration.
- **#416 (done)**: Multi-select status filtering on the admin Billing Events view (`payments/billing-events`). `status` on this endpoint is a derived value (`paid`/`failed`/`recorded`/`scheduled`, computed from `billing_events.event_type`, or hardcoded `scheduled` for projected future rows) rather than a stored column, so `GET /payments/billing-events` accepts `status` as a multi-value query param (repeated `status=a&status=b` or comma-separated, same `z.preprocess` normalizer pattern as #411's `lifecycle_status`) and filters the already-merged past+future array in JS before pagination; when the selected statuses exclude `scheduled`, the future-row DB query is skipped entirely rather than run and discarded. Admin UI: a `MultiSelectFilter` (#350) added to the existing filter bar, synced to a `?status=` URL param via the page's existing `pushParams`/`useSearchParams` pattern, so the filtered view is shareable/survives navigation. New `filter_status` i18n key in en/es/ca. `payments-billing-events.test.ts` extended with single/multi (comma and repeated-param)/invalid-value/tenant-isolation coverage for the new filter, plus a case confirming `status=scheduled` returns only virtual rows.
- **#446 (done)**: Expanding a nutrition template crashed with `TypeError: o.map is not a function`. #350 changed `GET /nutrition-library` from a bare array to `{ items, total, limit, offset }` (and `quality_slugs` → `qualities[]`); `NutritionPlanTree` still stored the paginated object and called `.map()` on it in `RestrictionsSection`. Tree now unwraps `items`, maps `qualities[].slug` → `quality_slugs`, and fetches `?limit=200`. No API/proxy change. Follow-up: admin and member `FeatureFlagsProvider` now wait for Clerk and send `Authorization` on `GET /api/proxy/feature-flags`, fixing the layout 401 (bare `fetch` with no token). Follow-up (this ticket): the `/class-types` 404 was the Schedule page (`[locale]/schedule/page.tsx`) still calling the pre-#360 `class-types` router (`class_type_id`/`class-types?status=active`), which was renamed to `activity-types` and now returns 404. Its `Session`/`ActivityType` types, form state, and API calls (fetch, `onActivityTypeChange`, session create/edit body) are now consistently `activity_type_id`/`/activity-types`, matching what `GET /class-sessions` actually returns and `POST/PUT /class-sessions` actually accept (both already `activity_type_id`, per the #360 stage 3 consolidation) — session create/edit was silently broken (400) on top of the dropdown 404. A separate, unreachable `[locale]/class-types/page.tsx` (not linked from any nav group, fully superseded by `[locale]/activity-types/`) still calls the same dead `/class-types` CRUD endpoints; left untouched as out-of-scope dead-code cleanup.
- **#476 (done)**: Activities expandable-row header aligned with the admin UI information hierarchy used elsewhere (Spaces reference pattern). Frontend-only — `activity-types.ts`'s `SELECT` already returned every field needed. `activity-types/page.tsx`: collapsed row header now shows Name/Created At/Created By/Status (`StatusBadge`)/Center/Trainer instead of Name/Description/Duration·Capacity/Center/Status; Duration/Capacity stay in the expanded General section (already present there). The expanded read-only view's `Metadata` section (Created/Modified By+At) is removed — `Created At`/`By` moved to the header, `Modified At`/`By` moved into the `Details` modal, which now also surfaces `Deleted At`/`By` and renders `status` via `StatusBadge`. No migration, no i18n changes (`col_created_at`/`col_created_by`/`col_default_trainer`/`meta_deleted_by`/`meta_deleted_at` already existed in en/es/ca, just unused). `docs/feature-patterns.md` updated to note Spaces and Activity Types as the documented exception to the "Details just expands the row" Inline-row-CRUD rule.
- **#362 (in progress — Slice 2 of Option A)**: Calendar functional audit found the page/data flow itself already correct (`apps/member/.../calendar/page.tsx` calls `GET /me/schedule` via the shared `apiClient` `buildHeaders()`-equivalent helper, defaults to `timeGridDay` (today), supports prev/next/today navigation, and has bounded loading + a `.catch` on fetch failures — no infinite-spinner bug here), but surfaced a real multi-center scoping gap: `GET /me/schedule` never applied any center restriction, so a member could see every center's class sessions in their gym instead of only their assigned center(s)'s. Root cause traced to `api/src/infra/centerContext.ts` resolving a `member`-role caller's `allowedCenterIds` via `member_centers JOIN members ON clerk_user_id = ctx.userId` — under member impersonation `userId` is the impersonating superadmin's own Clerk ID, never the impersonated member's, silently collapsing `allowedCenterIds` to `[]`. This underlying `centerContext` bug was independently fixed by #396; this PR wires `GET /me/schedule` in `api/src/api/me.ts` to call `getCenterContext(req)` and filter by `cs.center_id` (explicit `x-center-id` header, or restricted to `allowedCenterIds` for a plain member), mirroring the pattern already used by `centers.ts`/`class-sessions.ts`. Extended `api/src/test/me-impersonation-context.test.ts` with center-scoping regression coverage under impersonation. Bookings, Training, Nutrition, Membership/Payments, Alerts, and Profile audits remain as follow-up PRs against #362.
- **#360 (in progress — Stage 3: API consolidation)**: Unify Sessions and Events under `CalendarEvent`. Migration 134 closes schema gaps (adds `kind ENUM`, `center_id`, `modified_at`/`modified_by_membership_id` to `calendar_event_bookings`, fixes FK `fk_cestr_event` to CASCADE, adds `calendar_event_booking_id` to `class_package_transactions` and makes `booking_id` nullable). `class-sessions.ts` merged into `calendar-events.ts` (now exports both `classSessionsRouter` filtering `kind='session'` and `calendarEventsRouter` filtering `kind='event'`). `bookings.ts`, `package-credits.ts`, `plan-allowances.ts`, `shared-training-requests.ts`, and all 9 `/me/*` calendar/booking/shared-training endpoints repointed from `class_sessions`+`bookings`+`shared_training_requests` to `calendar_events`+`calendar_event_bookings`+`calendar_event_shared_training_requests`. `scheduleEngine.ts` extended to write `kind='session'`, `capacity`, and `center_id` on materialized events. `app.ts` import updated; old `class-sessions.ts` deleted. TypeScript clean; DB tests blocked by local env (ER_ACCESS_DENIED). Stages 4 (frontend) and 5 (cleanup: drop old tables, remove legacy columns) remain.
- **#362 (in progress — Slice 3 of Option A)**: Bookings functional audit. `apps/member/.../schedule/page.tsx` ("My Bookings") only ever fetched `/me/schedule` bounded to the next 14 days, so it could show upcoming bookable/booked sessions but had no way to show past bookings at all, and no way to open/view a booking's fuller details — despite the backend already having purpose-built `GET /me/upcoming`/`GET /me/activity-history` endpoints from #194 that nothing in the frontend ever called. Wired a new "Past bookings" section into the page using `GET /me/activity-history` (own loading/empty/error state, tap-to-expand showing attendance/cancellation detail); added the same tap-to-expand to the existing upcoming list (showing description/capacity) — this is the "open/view a booking" the ticket asks for, since there's no member-facing single-booking-by-id backend route to build a separate detail page against. Also fixed the page's error/empty-state conflation: a failed `/me/schedule` fetch used to set the same `message` state as a successful booking action, so an error banner and the redundant "No upcoming sessions" empty text could show together — `loadError` is now tracked separately and suppresses the empty-state text. Backend bugs found and fixed alongside: `GET /me/bookings` joined the pre-migration-059 `class_types`/`cs.class_type_id` columns (dropped on a fully-migrated DB) and 500'd on every call — rewritten to join `activity_types`/`activity_type_id` like every other `/me/*` handler; `GET /me/bookings`, `GET /me/upcoming`, and `GET /me/activity-history` never applied any center scoping (unlike the already-fixed `/me/schedule`) — all three now call `getCenterContext(req)` and filter by `cs.center_id`, matching the pattern fixed for `/me/schedule` in Slice 2. Cancellation (`DELETE /me/bookings/:id`) was already correctly implemented and member-reachable — confirmed working, not rebuilt. New `api/src/test/me-bookings.test.ts` covers auth, impersonation identity resolution, and center scoping for all three endpoints, plus the fixed `activity_types` join. Training, Nutrition, Membership/Payments, Alerts, and Profile audits remain as follow-up PRs against #362.
- **#364 (done)**: Made Admin staff impersonation fully database-driven — `api/src/api/impersonation.ts` no longer imports/instantiates `createClerkClient` at all. `GET /platform/impersonation/targets` builds the staff target list straight from `gym_memberships` (no per-row `clerkClient.users.getUser()` lookup, so a staff member missing from Clerk still appears). `POST /platform/impersonation/:targetId` validates staff targets against `gym_memberships` only (no Clerk superadmin check on the target). Per the ticket's answered clarification, no new `superadmins` DB table was introduced: superadmins are excluded implicitly because they carry no `gym_memberships` row (`TenantContext.gymMembershipId` is already documented as `null` for superadmins) — impersonating a superadmin's Clerk ID fails the same "no membership in this gym" check as any other non-staff ID. `requireSuperadmin` (gating who may *call* these endpoints) is unchanged and still Clerk-based. `impersonation.test.ts` updated: replaced the old "excludes staff who are superadmins" mock-driven tests with tests proving staff discovery/validation succeed even when the Clerk `getUser` mock throws for that user.
- **#372 (done)**: Member detail "Session Packages" section + attendance/cancellation-driven package-credit consumption, per the ticket's answered business-rule table. `MemberExpandedRow.tsx` (admin Members page) now renders each of a member's `user_class_packages` rows (name, purchased date, total/used/remaining sessions, expiration, status) via the existing `GET /members/:memberId/class-packages`; PAYMENTS-write staff get an inline "Extend expiration" action against a new `PUT /members/:memberId/class-packages/:id/extend-expiration` (400 unless the new date is strictly later, audited via `recordAudit` + new `user_class_packages.modified_at`/`modified_by_membership_id` columns, migration 128). Credit-consumption logic in `package-credits.ts`/`bookings.ts` changed: `cancelBooking` now only auto-refunds a spent credit when the member cancels **at least 1 day before** the session (`DATEDIFF(session.starts_at, UTC_TIMESTAMP()) >= 1`) — a same-day cancellation keeps the credit consumed by default, matching the confirmed rule that a same-day cancel or a booked-then-absent no-show still counts as consumption. New `POST /bookings/:id/refund-credit` (requireRole admin/front_desk/trainer_performance/trainer_perf_nutrition) lets a trainer explicitly refund a same-day-cancelled booking's credit — gated on `bookings.user_class_package_id` still being set, so calling it twice 400s instead of double-refunding. New `POST /class-sessions/:id/walk-in` (same roles) covers the "no booking, but attended" case from the rule table: books the member (`bookMemberOnSession(force=true)`, reusing the existing booking-time debit) and marks them present in one transaction, rejecting with 409 if they already have an active booking for that session. `refundPackageCredit` gained `reason`/`actorUserId` params so the ledger (`class_package_transactions`) distinguishes an automatic advance-cancellation refund from a manual same-day one. New `api/src/test/package-credits.test.ts` covers all of the above plus tenant isolation and role gating.
- **#374 (done)**: Support Multi-Member Membership Plans. Migration 129 adds `membership_plans.member_limit` (`'1'`/`'2'`/`'family'`, named CHECK, default `'1'`) and a new `user_membership_members` join table (gym_id, `user_membership_id`/`member_id` FKs both `ON DELETE CASCADE`, `is_owner`, UNIQUE on the pair) recording every Member covered by a Membership; existing Memberships are backfilled so their current owner becomes their sole covered Member (equivalent to `member_limit='1'`), so no manual data migration is needed. `membership-plans.ts` validates `member_limit` on create/update, copies it on duplicate, and rejects (400) shrinking it below the covered-Member count of any active Membership already on that plan. `user-memberships.ts`: `POST /` now inserts the paying Member as `is_owner=1` into `user_membership_members` in the same transaction as the Membership row; new `GET/POST /:id/members` and `DELETE /:id/members/:memberId` (all `requireModuleWrite('PAYMENTS')` for writes) manage the covered-Member list, enforcing the plan's cap server-side and protecting the owner from removal. Per the ticket's "benefits apply to all covered Members" requirement, the plan's booking-time entitlement checks (`plan-allowances.ts` and `plan-class-types.ts`'s `registerBookingAccessHook`s) and `GET /me/schedule`'s `access_locked` flag were extended so a covered Member gets the same access as the Membership's owner, not just the paying Member. Admin UI: Plans page gets a "Members per Membership" select (Individual/Couple/Family) in the add/edit/details views; Memberships page gets a new "Members" action opening `MembershipMembersModal.tsx` (owner-first list, add via a Member picker respecting the cap, remove for non-owners). Bug fix found and fixed along the way (pre-existing, unrelated to this ticket): `user-memberships.ts`'s `effectivePrice()` selected `membership_plans.base_price`, a column dropped in migration 058 and never restored — every `POST /user-memberships` call was failing with a MySQL "Unknown column" error, apparently uncaught because this router had zero test coverage before now. New `api/src/test/user-memberships.test.ts` (the router's first test file — auth/role/tenant-isolation guards, full CRUD happy paths, and the whole `/:id/members` sub-resource including cap enforcement for each `member_limit` value, the owner-removal guard, and the duplicate-member 409) and extensions to `membership-plans.test.ts` for `member_limit` validation and the shrink-guard.
- **#375 (done)**: Aligned Membership Plans with the standard Admin Inline row CRUD pattern (frontend-only; no migration, no API changes). `apps/admin/src/app/[locale]/plans/page.tsx` no longer imports `CrudModal` — the Add Plan, Details, Price, Allowance and Centers modals were all removed. `+ Add Plan` now opens an inline creation row (`inlineNew` state, `renderInlineNewRow()`, capturing Name/Description/Status/Enrollment as in the ticket's mock; a default `billing_policies` row is created alongside it, same as before). `Details` from the row's `ContextMenu` now just expands the row read-only (`showDetails()`) instead of opening a separate modal, satisfying the ticket's "Details and Edit use the same expanded context" requirement. Billing Policy, Centers, Included Services (allowances) and Price History each gained a per-section inline `Edit`/`+ Add` toggle within the expanded row (`billingEditForPlanId`, `centersForPlanId`, `allowanceForPlanId`, `priceForPlanId`), matching the pre-existing inline Charge Benefits editor already on this page — this also newly makes Billing Policy editable after creation (previously only settable via the removed Add modal). `docs/feature-patterns.md` and `CLAUDE.md` updated: Plans is now the **Inline row CRUD** reference (alongside Products/Taxes) instead of **Modal CRUD**; Class Types is the new Modal CRUD reference. Also carries forward #374's "Members per Membership" select and Members-action wiring on the Plans/Memberships pages, reconciled with the inline-row rewrite during the merge with #374.
- **#436 (done)**: Fixed "Delete Center" (`DELETE /centers/:id`), which crashed with a 500 on almost every real-world delete. Root cause: `firstDependentTable()`'s pre-delete dependency guard queried a table called `events`, permanently dropped in migration `095_drop_events.js` (#221) — the guard only reaches that entry when a center has no dependents in the tables checked before it, so it fired on the common case (deleting a center with no remaining dependents), not the rare one. Also modernized two other stale entries left over from the #360 stage 3 consolidation: `class_sessions` → `calendar_events` (scoped to `kind = 'session'`, so one-off Activities/CalendarEvents don't block a delete) and `bookings` → `calendar_event_bookings`. Follow-up after CI on #433: `rooms` → `spaces` (migration 067 rename), which #436 had left in place and which still 500'd every clean delete; session test fixtures now set required `calendar_events.title`; `POST /me/shared-training-requests` aliases `class_session_id` again. Soft delete (`deleted_at`/`deleted_by_membership_id`/`deleted_by_name`), audit logging (`recordAudit`, entity type `center`), and recycle-bin integration (`center` was already a registered `EntityType` in `recycle-bin.ts`) were all already correct — they just needed the crash removed so the DELETE handler could reach them. First test coverage for this router: new `api/src/test/centers.test.ts` (auth/role/tenant-isolation guards, full CRUD happy paths, and the dependency-check/soft-delete/audit/recycle-bin-restore regression this fix addresses).
- **#376 (done)**: Assign Plan to Member(s) and Create Membership & Billing Events, building on #374's multi-member model. New `POST /membership-plans/:id/assign` (`api/src/api/membership-plans.ts`, admin-only) takes `{ member_ids[], owner_member_id, starts_at }`, validates the count against the plan's `member_limit` (exactly 1/2, unlimited for `family`) and that every member exists in the gym, then in one transaction: inserts the `user_memberships` row (owner as `member_id`, reusing `effectivePrice()` from `user-memberships.ts`), writes the P1.6 `status_changed` billing ledger row via `recordStatusChange`, inserts one `user_membership_members` row per selected member (owner flagged, per #374), and snapshots the plan's current `plan_charge_benefits` onto a new `user_membership_charge_benefits` table (migration 130 — same shape as `plan_charge_benefits`, `no_benefit` rows excluded, unique on `(user_membership_id, gym_charge_id)`) so a later edit to the Plan's benefits never retroactively changes an already-created Membership. After the transaction commits, any Promotion currently targeting the plan is auto-applied best-effort via the existing `applyPromotionToMembership()` (a non-stackable conflict is skipped, not fatal). Reuses the existing `ER_DUP_ENTRY` → 409 handling for "member already has an active membership." Admin Plans page gained an "Assign Plan to Member" context-menu action opening `AssignPlanModal.tsx` (member search/multi-select via the existing `MemberSearchInput`, owner radio-select, capacity warning, starts-on date).
- **#438 (done)**: Fixed feature-flag/Home sync in `apps/member/`. The "Next Booking" section on Home (`page.tsx`) rendered unconditionally regardless of the `member_web.my_bookings` flag, because it's backed by `GET /me/schedule` — deliberately left ungated at the API layer (#367) since it's shared with the un-flagged `/calendar` page — so disabling the flag left the widget (and its backend-gated `DELETE /me/bookings/:id` cancel button) visibly stuck instead of hidden; now explicitly wrapped in the same `featureEnabled('member_web.my_bookings')` check (superadmin-bypassing) already used by the nav tiles and My Membership card. Also fixed client-side staleness: `FeatureFlagsProvider` fetched flags once on mount and never again, so an admin's toggle wasn't reflected in an already-open member session without a full reload — it now polls `GET /feature-flags` every 30s, matching the backend's in-memory cache TTL (`api/src/infra/featureFlags.ts`). Scoped to `apps/member/` per the ticket; the identical staleness pattern in `apps/admin/`'s `FeatureFlagsContext.tsx`, plus impersonation-scoped flag evaluation, is covered by #439.
- **#360 (in progress — stage 3 of 5, API consolidation)**: Unify Sessions and Events under `CalendarEvent`, staged per the agreed rollout (design → schema+booking support → API consolidation → frontend consolidation → cleanup; stages 1–2 landed via #406/#407). This stage repoints every booking/session/shared-training router at the stage-2 schema with no behavior or field-name change visible to any frontend: `class-sessions.ts` merged into `calendar-events.ts` (still mounted at `/class-sessions` and `/calendar-events`, discriminated by a new `calendar_events.kind ENUM('session','event')` column so the admin Calendar page's existing dual-fetch, #326, still renders each occurrence exactly once); `bookings.ts`, `me.ts`, `shared-training-requests.ts`, `plan-allowances.ts`, and `package-credits.ts` now read/write `calendar_events`/`calendar_event_bookings`/`calendar_event_shared_training_requests` instead of `class_sessions`/`bookings`/`shared_training_requests` (now frozen, dropped in stage 5). Migration 134 closes schema gaps found while scoping this stage: `calendar_events.center_id` (was never added despite docs assuming it existed), `calendar_event_bookings.center_id`/`modified_at`/`modified_by_membership_id`, `calendar_event_shared_training_requests`'s FK recreated `ON DELETE CASCADE`, and `class_package_transactions.calendar_event_booking_id` (a mutual-exclusivity CHECK against the now-legacy `booking_id`). Also fixed a pre-existing bug found along the way: `domain/scheduleEngine.ts`'s schedule-rule materializer fetched `activity_type.max_capacity` but never wrote it to the generated `calendar_events.capacity` column — now backfilled for existing rows and fixed going forward, and the materializer also resolves `center_id` (activity type's `default_center_id`, falling back to the gym's sole center) and stamps `kind='session'`. Per the ticket's answered scoping questions (issue comment thread), this was a hard cutover with no dual-write/backfill (no production data to preserve) and no literal router-file merge deferral to stage 4. See `docs/architecture.md`'s "Planned: CalendarEvent Unification" section and `docs/decisions.md` #10. Frontend consolidation (stage 4) and dropping the old tables (stage 5) remain.
- **#417 (done — Stage 3: nutrition meal images)**: Cloudflare R2 image storage integration, per the agreed 3-stage plan on the issue thread (Stage 1: R2 client + bucket-init action, no upload yet; Stage 2: generic upload endpoint + rewire `exercises.image_url`; Stage 3: add `image_url` to nutrition meals). Credentials are platform-wide env vars only (`CLOUDFLARE_R2_ENDPOINT`/`CLOUDFLARE_R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID`/`CLOUDFLARE_R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`/`CLOUDFLARE_R2_BUCKET`) — one shared bucket, folder-scoped per gym — not per-gym DB-stored secrets, per the issue's confirmed resolution to CLAUDE.md's "no hardcoded values"/"no additional stores" constraints. New `api/src/infra/storage.ts` lazily constructs an `@aws-sdk/client-s3` client (mirrors `payments/index.ts`'s `getPaymentProvider()` pattern) and exposes `isStorageConfigured()`, `sanitizeGymFolderName()`/`buildGymFolderPrefix()`, and `initializeGymBucket()` (creates the `Nutrition/Images`, `Exercises/Images`, `Exercises/Videos`, `Branding/Logo`, `Branding/Images`, `Members` folder markers). Migration 135 adds `gyms.storage_folder_prefix` (nullable, unique) and `gyms.storage_initialized_at` (nullable). New `POST /platform/gyms/:id/storage/initialize` (superadmin) — 503 if the deployment isn't configured, 502 on an R2 failure, idempotent (reuses the captured prefix on re-run). Admin **Cordel → Gyms** page gets a read-only Storage section + "Initialize Cloudflare Bucket" button. Follow-up fix: the GitHub Environment `dev` secrets/vars for R2 existed but `deploy.yml` never forwarded them to the VPS, so `isStorageConfigured()` was always false in production and the button never rendered (only the "not configured" warning did) — `deploy.yml` now passes all four `CLOUDFLARE_R2_*` values through. This also came with a deploy-mechanism change: the deploy script now writes the API's whole runtime env — DB creds, Clerk keys, and the R2 values — as inline `Environment=` lines directly in the `fitness-api.container` quadlet unit (regenerated every deploy, GitHub stays the source of truth), not a separate `fitness-api.env` file as before. **Stage 2**: new `api/src/api/storage.ts` (`storageRouter`, mounted at `/storage`) exposes `POST /storage/uploads/exercise-image` (`requireModuleWrite('TRAINING')` + `requireFeatureEnabled('training.exercises')`) — one route per upload target so each target keeps its own module/feature guard, rather than one shared gate. Raw-body upload via `express.raw({ type: image/*, limit: '6mb' })`, same pattern as the existing theme-logo upload in `themes.ts`; validates mime (png/jpeg/webp/gif, else 415) and a 5MB size cap (413); 503 if the deployment has no R2 configured; 409 with the ticket's exact copy ("Cloudflare storage has not been initialized for this gym, therefore images cannot be uploaded.") if the calling gym's `storage_folder_prefix` is still null; 502 on an R2 failure. New `infra/storage.ts` `uploadGymImage()` builds the object key as `<folderPrefix>/<folder>/<uuid>.<ext>` and returns the URL as `${CLOUDFLARE_R2_ENDPOINT}/${CLOUDFLARE_R2_BUCKET}/<key>`, per the issue thread's confirmed URL-construction answer. Frontend: new reusable `apps/admin/src/components/ImageUploadField.tsx` (thumbnail preview + Upload/Replace/Remove buttons + inline not-configured/not-initialized warning, reading two new fields — `storage_configured`/`storage_folder_prefix` — added to `GymContext`'s `GymOption`) replaces the plain URL `<input>` for `image_url` in both the Exercises inline-edit row and the Add-Exercise modal; the read-only Exercise Details modal and the expanded-row view now render the image as a thumbnail instead of a raw URL string. **Stage 3**: migration 138 adds nullable `nutrition_library_items.image_url` (`VARCHAR(500)`, mirrors `exercises.image_url`) — gym-owned items only, since system (`gym_id IS NULL`) catalog items have no per-gym storage folder to upload into. New sibling route `POST /storage/uploads/nutrition-image` (`requireModuleWrite('NUTRITION')` + `requireFeatureEnabled('nutrition.nutrition_library')`) shares `handleImageUpload()` with the exercise-image route, uploading into `Nutrition/Images`. `nutrition-library.ts`'s create/update endpoints now accept/return `image_url` (the platform/system-item router, `platform-nutrition-library.ts`, is untouched — system items stay image-less). Admin **Nutrition → Nutrition Library** page's Add/Edit modals use the shared `ImageUploadField` (`uploadPath="/storage/uploads/nutrition-image"`), and the expanded row's detail view renders the stored image as a thumbnail when set. Follow-up: the Initialize Cloudflare Bucket button is always visible in Cordel → Gyms (disabled + warning when R2 env vars aren't set, rather than hidden); `initializeGymBucket()` also writes parent folder markers (`<prefix>/`, `Nutrition/`, `Exercises/`, `Branding/`) so the R2 tree matches the ticket exactly. Second follow-up (per issue thread): the button is now always **enabled** too (not just visible) — the "not configured for this deployment" warning still shows, but clicking still works so the folder structure can be (re)created later once R2 is configured, or to add new folders in the future; a misconfigured deployment surfaces the existing 503 inline via the toast instead of being blocked ahead of time. Third follow-up (#542): the 503 from both `POST /platform/gyms/:id/storage/initialize` and `POST /storage/uploads/:target` now names exactly which `CLOUDFLARE_R2_*` env var(s) are unset (new `getMissingStorageConfigKeys()` in `infra/storage.ts`, returned as both a detailed `error` string and a `missingConfig` array) instead of a single generic "not configured" message — surfaced automatically wherever the existing toast/inline-error UI already renders `err.message`, no frontend change needed. Fourth follow-up (#542, per the issue thread's "I'd need a bit more of information/traces"): R2 failures now return structured diagnostics instead of a single toast line. `infra/storage.ts` gains `getStorageDiagnostics()` (endpoint split into protocol/host/path + `endpointMalformed`, bucket, `missingConfig`, and credential *lengths* only — never the access key or secret), `describeStorageError()` (flattens an `@aws-sdk/client-s3` rejection: error `name`/`code`, `$metadata.httpStatusCode`/`requestId`/`attempts`, and the `err.cause` chain) and a `StorageOperationError` wrapper that names the exact object key that failed. Both the 503 and the 502 from `POST /platform/gyms/:id/storage/initialize` now carry `diagnostics`; the 502 also carries `details`. The upload route's 502 carries `details` only — it is gym-staff-facing, so the deployment-config snapshot goes to the pino log rather than the response. Admin **Cordel → Gyms** pins a dismissible failure panel under the button (all fields + a "Copy diagnostics" button) so the report survives longer than a toast. Also fixes a likely root cause of the reported failure: the `S3Client` now pins `requestChecksumCalculation`/`responseChecksumValidation` to `WHEN_REQUIRED`, since aws-sdk-js v3 ≥ 3.729 defaults to `WHEN_SUPPORTED` and sends a trailing CRC32 checksum with `content-encoding: aws-chunked`, which R2 rejects.
- **#439 (done)**: Feature flag toggles didn't reach an already-open admin/member session (fetched once on mount, no polling), and a superadmin's flag-bypass incorrectly carried over while impersonating, so an impersonated user saw the superadmin's unrestricted view instead of their own. Fix: `requireFeatureEnabled` (`api/src/infra/featureFlags.ts`) now bypasses only in native capacity — `tenantCtx.isSuperadmin && !tenantCtx.impersonatedUserId` — `tenantCtx.isSuperadmin` itself is unchanged (still `true` under impersonation for other permission checks). Both `FeatureFlagsContext.tsx` (admin and member) now poll every 30s, matching the backend's in-memory cache TTL. Frontend bypass checks were updated the same way: admin's `Sidebar.tsx` computes `filterNavGroups`'s role as `(isSuperadmin && !isImpersonating) ? 'superadmin' : activeGym?.role`; member's Home/`/training`/`/nutrition`/`/schedule`/`/membership`/`/profile` use `isSuperadmin && !isImpersonating` in place of raw `isSuperadmin`, `isImpersonating` from `useImpersonation()`. `AdminBar`/`TopHeader`'s own impersonate/exit-impersonation controls and `GymSwitcher`'s pre-impersonation gym picker intentionally keep raw `isSuperadmin` — they must render in native capacity regardless of feature flags. Extended `member-web-feature-flags.test.ts` to assert the corrected precedence (impersonated superadmin gets 403 when the flag is off; native superadmin still bypasses).
- **#415 (done)**: Member-app Home froze under admin impersonation (Next Booking / My Membership stuck on Loading, Calendar and other tiles appearing unresponsive). Network evidence: `GET /me/profile` 403 and `GET /feature-flags` 401. Root cause: a bare superadmin has no member identity — `/me/profile` is `requireRole('member')` so `tenantContext` assigns `role: 'admin'` and 403s; Home's local `loading` only clears inside `load()`, which is gated on `isLinked`, so widgets spun forever and other pages bounced home. Impersonation hydration from `sessionStorage` also lagged `AppContext`'s first fetch, so a refresh while impersonating could fire the same 403 before the `x-impersonate-as` header was restored. Fix: `ImpersonationProvider` exposes `ready` and `AppContext` waits on it; skip `/me/profile` until impersonating; prefer `session.gymId`; Home shows a Support-mode prompt instead of infinite spinners; feature-flag fetch requires a Clerk token; member impersonation dialog lists members only. Tests: `GET /me/profile` 403 as bare superadmin, 200 under `x-impersonate-as: member:<id>`.
- **#559 (done — stages 1–4)**: Calendar styling section for Base Themes and Custom Themes. **Stage 4** closes the ticket with the accessibility pass: a WCAG-2.1 contrast report under the Calendar section in both theme editors (`apps/admin/src/lib/calendarContrast.ts` + `CalendarContrastReport` in `ThemeTokensEditor.tsx`) that scores every foreground/surface pair the calendar paints — advisory only, never a save blocker, since a gym's branding is its own and `validateTokens()` stays a pure format check; `:focus-visible` indicators for calendar events and nav buttons drawn from tokens guaranteed to read against their surroundings (added identically to Admin's and Member Web's `CalendarThemeStyles.tsx`); and `calendarVarValue()` in both `themeTokens.ts` files hardening the fallback so an *unusable* persisted value (empty or non-hex) falls back to the default rather than dropping the CSS declaration. Covered by `apps/admin/src/test/calendar-a11y.test.ts` and Member Web mirror checks. **Stage 3** finished the event surfaces: an event's fill no longer encodes its status — every event takes the theme's new `calendarEventBackground`/`calendarEventBorder` (joining `calendarEventText`) plus a new `calendarEventHoverBackground` advanced attribute, and the booking status moves to a pill-shaped badge rendered inside the event (`components/CalendarStatusBadge.tsx`, the shape and palette of the app's existing `StatusBadge`). That is the resolution of the conflict with #541 agreed in the thread: status still decides a color and nothing else does, it just paints the badge rather than the box, which is what frees the box to be themeable. `lib/calendarEventColors.ts` remains the single status → color mapping, now returning a `{ bg, fg }` pair and covering `full` (a `scheduled` session at capacity), and the badge labels are translated from `calendar.status_*` instead of showing the raw uppercased DB value. The admin calendar page stops setting a per-event `backgroundColor`/`borderColor` (FullCalendar writes those inline, which would beat the theme); the sheet maps the tokens onto `--fc-event-bg-color`/`--fc-event-border-color`/`--fc-event-text-color` and adds the radius (re-asserting FullCalendar's corner-flattening for continued multi-day events), a hover background and the selected/focused overlay at `opacity: .25`. Member Web is deliberately unchanged — its events are colored by availability to that member via an inline style that wins over these rules. Defaults are the `#6c63ff` purple `scheduled` events already had, so an unconfigured calendar looks as before. **Stage 2** made the stage-1 tokens visible: `applyTokens()` (both `apps/admin/src/lib/themeTokens.ts` and its Member Web mirror) writes one `--gd-calendar-*` CSS variable per calendar token — driven by the new `CALENDAR_COLOR_VARS`/`CALENDAR_ADVANCED_VARS` maps, with every key falling back to its default so a pre-#559 theme still resolves the full set — and a new `CalendarThemeStyles` component holds the FullCalendar override sheet that reads them. The sheet maps each token onto FullCalendar v6's own `--fc-*` variable where one exists (border, page/today/highlight/non-business, the whole nav-button palette), so one declaration reaches `timeGridDay`/`timeGridWeek`/`dayGridMonth` at once, and adds an explicit rule for the surfaces FullCalendar hardcodes (column headers, month-view date numbers including other-month dates, the time axis, slot height, weekend columns). Every rule is scoped to a `gd-calendar` wrapper class on the calendar container in both the admin and member calendar pages, and every `var()` repeats the default token value as its CSS fallback, so an unthemed page renders exactly as before. Event colors are untouched — the three event variables are emitted but read by no rule yet. Two intentional pixel-level changes: other-month dates now use `calendarMutedDayText` at full opacity instead of FullCalendar's `opacity: .3`, and non-business slots paint the configured `calendarDisabledSlotBackground` opaquely instead of FullCalendar's translucent grey (the stage-1 default, `#f7f7f7`, is what that grey renders as over white). Stage 1 added the token layer and the editor section only — 16 `calendar*` colors on `tokens.colors` plus 5 `calendar*` entries in the `advanced` map, their defaults (mirroring FullCalendar's own built-ins so an unconfigured theme is visually unchanged), hex validation in `api/src/domain/themeTokens.ts` (`CALENDAR_COLOR_FIELDS`), and a new `group_calendar` entry in `COLOR_GROUPS`, which gives both theme editors the section at once since they share `ThemeColorsEditor`. No migration: the tokens blob is schema-less JSON and missing keys fall back to defaults, so pre-#559 themes keep saving and rendering unchanged. Per the ticket's clarification thread the Base↔Custom relationship is deliberately untouched — cloning copies the calendar tokens along with everything else, exactly as before; no live sparse inheritance was introduced.
- **#547 (done)**: Membership Plan Pricing section — one current, VAT-inclusive price per plan plus an immutable price history, per the ticket thread's final clarification ("Price field should be fixed and aligned with the behavior that we have in products… Add Price need to be removed… Save… Apply new price to assigned plans"). Earlier rounds shipped the tax model (#413) and the live net/gross recalculation (#581); this closes the ticket. Migration 161 adds `membership_plan_prices.status` (`active`/`applied`/`inactive`, named CHECK), `applied_at`, and a `tax_rate_id`/`tax_rate_percent` VAT snapshot per price row, backfilled from the window covering today and the plan's current tax rate. New `PUT /membership-plans/:id/pricing` (admin) replaces the plan's current price in one transaction: the superseded row is closed (`valid_to` = yesterday, or its own `valid_from` when it opened today) and marked `inactive`, a new open-ended `active` row opens today with the selected VAT snapshotted, and the plan's `tax_rate_id` is updated with `tax_behavior` pinned to `inclusive` — a VAT change opens a new price too, and re-saving identical values is a no-op. New `POST /membership-plans/:id/pricing/apply-to-assigned-plans` (admin) pushes the current price onto the `user_memberships` still running on the plan (`draft`/`awaiting_payment`/`active`/`paused` only) and marks the price `applied`; terminal (cancelled/expired) Assigned Plans and already-generated `billing_events` are never touched (req. 15), and an Assigned Plan with a `discount_reason` keeps its agreed `final_price` while its plan-price snapshot is refreshed — the response reports `{ updated, kept_discounted }`. `recomputePriceStatuses()` re-derives the status column from the validity windows after every price write (including the legacy `POST`/`PUT`/`DELETE /:id/prices` endpoints, kept for API compatibility), so status can never drift from the dates it describes; `effectivePrice()` and `enrichPlan()` break a same-day tie in favour of the non-history row. Admin UI: the expanded Plan row's "Add Price" button and its from/to price-window sub-form are gone, replaced by a **Pricing** section (Price (VAT included) + Tax Rate + **Save**, with the live net/gross preview) and an **Apply new price to assigned plans** button behind a `ConfirmDialog`; the **Price History** section below it is read-only and badges each row Active / Applied to assigned plans / Inactive. Tax Rate and Tax Behavior were removed from the plan's inline edit form (price and VAT are edited together in Pricing, and a plan's price is always VAT-inclusive). Duplication copies each price with its VAT snapshot but never the `applied` marker. Tests: 18 new cases in `api/src/test/membership-plans.test.ts` (auth/role, tenant isolation, cross-tenant tax rate, validation, 0%/21%/10% rates, history immutability, apply-to-assigned including the discount and cancelled-membership cases, duplication) and the updated `plans-pricing-locales.test.ts` key set.
- **Training module redesign (#60–#63, done)**: dynamic Workout Block form driven by
  `blockFieldConfig.ts` (#60); tree-grid Training Plan Template editor (#61); dependency
  awareness for shared catalog entities (#62); tree-grid Workout Template editor with
  in-place block/exercise CRUD, paginated/filtered list (`created_by_membership_id`,
  migration 053) and cross-template block drag-and-drop via `PUT …/blocks/:id/move` (#63).
  Patterns documented in `docs/feature-patterns.md` (Tree-Grid Editor, Config-Driven Fields,
  Dependency Awareness).
- **Personalized Training Plans module (#67, done)**: gym-level **Training → Training
  Plans** page (list, filters by member, source template, status, created by, and date
  ranges) with a two-step "New Training Plan" dialog (From Template / From Scratch) that
  posts to `POST /training-plans`. Existing-active-plan collisions return `409`; the UI
  swaps to a keep-vs-expire prompt whose "Expire" branch closes prior actives
  (`status='expired'`, `end_date` = new plan's start) and their `member_training_plans`
  rows atomically before the new plan is created. Editor page at
  `/training-plans/[id]` with a header form (name/desc/dates/status), drag-reorder for
  workouts, cross-parent move + duplicate for blocks and exercises via new endpoints on
  `training-plans.ts` (`…/blocks/:id/move`, `…/exercises/:id/move`,
  `…/duplicate` at each level). Templates page gains an **Assign Plan to Member** action
  on active templates that opens the same dialog with the template preselected.
  Migration 054 adds `training_plans.start_date` (backfilled from the latest
  `member_training_plans.valid_from`) and `end_date`, and updates the status CHECK to
  `draft/active/expired/deleted` (existing `inactive` rows remapped to `expired`).
  `training-plan-creation.ts` extracts the clone/write-history transaction so both
  `POST /training-plans` and the legacy `POST /members/:id/member-training-plans` share
  one implementation.
- **Training Plans UX Redesign — Inline Editing (#112, done)**: replaced the
  `/training-plans/[id]` editor page with an inline expandable card list matching the
  Training Plan Templates UX. Each card expands to show workouts/blocks/exercises with
  the existing drag-reorder and block/exercise CRUD modals. New context menu actions:
  **Edit** (inline metadata), **Details** (read-only modal with full audit info),
  **Duplicate** (clones the full plan tree to a draft), and **Complete** (transitions
  status to `completed`, stamps `end_date`, makes plan read-only). Migration 066 adds
  `completed` to the status CHECK constraint. The old `/training-plans/[id]` route now
  redirects to the list. All write endpoints on `training-plans.ts` reject completed plans
  with 403.
- **Training Plan Workout Builder Parity (#164, done)**: Training Plan workout cards now have
  full UX parity with the Workout Template builder. Extracted `WorkoutBlockBuilder.tsx`
  (`apps/admin/src/app/[locale]/workout-templates/`) — a self-contained component with its own
  dnd-kit `DndContext` that handles block reorder, exercise reorder within a block, and
  cross-block exercise moves via `PUT …/blocks/:srcBlockId/exercises/:exId/move`. Props:
  `workoutKey` (namespaces drag IDs to avoid collision across multiple open workout cards),
  `blocksUrl`, `blocks: HierBlock[]`, `canWrite`, `onChanged`. Training Plans page updated:
  block/exercise CRUD modals removed from the workout tree; each workout card now renders
  `WorkoutBlockBuilder` inline with a searchable `ExerciseCombobox`, always-editable fields
  (inline block name + type + config, inline exercise rows, result_type/target_value/unit),
  block Duplicate/Delete context menus, and drag-and-drop at both levels. Workout name
  always-editable inline (on-blur PUT, reverts on error). `+ Add Workout` replaces
  `window.prompt()` with an inline form. `canWrite` now uses `canWriteModule(role, 'TRAINING')`
  (matches Training Plan Templates pattern). No API or DB changes — backend already supported
  all required endpoints (`/blocks`, `/blocks/:id/exercises`, cross-block `/move`, etc.).
- **Billing ledger member history endpoint (#114, done)**: `GET /billing-events/member/:memberId`
  convenience alias added to `billing-events.ts` (paginated, tenant-isolated, 404 on unknown
  member). First test suite for the billing ledger (`billing-events.test.ts`): 14 tests
  covering auth, happy paths, new endpoint, and cross-gym tenant isolation.
- **Cordel platform menu + two-scope audit log (#66, done)**: superadmin-only **Cordel**
  sidebar group (Gyms, Users, platform Audit Log at `/cordel/audit`); **System** reduced to
  gym-scoped Audit Log + Customize and opened to gym admins (`requiredRole: 'admin'`).
  `GET /audit-logs?scope=all` (platform superadmins only, `tenantCtx.isSuperadmin`) lifts
  the tenant gym filter and joins `gyms.name`; shared `AuditLogView` component renders both
  scopes, adding a Gym column in platform mode.
- **Audit Log UX Improvements (#69, done)**: each audit row is now a self-contained
  historical snapshot. `actor_name` and `entity_name` are captured at write time and never
  recalculated (migration 055; existing rows truncated). Actor name comes from the Clerk user
  object already fetched in `tenantContext` (zero extra queries). Entity name is resolved by
  `infra/audit-registry.ts`: named entities use a `name`-column lookup; M-N/link entities
  get a composed label from their FK parents (e.g. `user_membership` → member name + plan
  name). FK fields in `previous_values`/`new_values` are enriched at write time from plain
  `foo_id: value` to `foo: { id, name }` objects. `GET /audit-logs/meta` returns the full
  entity-type list + action list for dropdown population. New filter params: `entity_name`
  (LIKE), `actor` (name LIKE or Clerk ID exact match), `action`, `source`. `AuditLogView`
  gains dropdowns for entity type, action and source; actor now searched by stored name;
  entity column shows `type#id` + name beneath it; Clerk uid surfaced only in the expanded
  row. `class-types.ts` and `promotions.ts` gain `recordAudit` coverage.
- **Done outside the phase plan**:
  - **Assign Members When Creating a CalendarEvent (#366)**: staff can assign one or more Members directly when creating a session or a recurring activity, instead of booking them one at a time afterward. `POST /class-sessions` accepts an optional `member_ids: number[]`, validated and booked (via the existing `bookMemberOnSession(force=true)` — same mechanism as a manual staff "Add member") inside the same transaction as the session's own INSERT. New `activity_type_schedule_rule_members` table (migration 135) lets staff assign Members to a recurring `activity_type_schedule_rules` row; `materializeScheduleRule` auto-books every assigned Member on each freshly generated `calendar_events` occurrence — including occurrences generated by a later `PUT` re-materialization — so the assignment isn't a one-time snapshot. Capacity is always advisory for this staff-driven path (never blocks create/save); Member-facing privacy needed no changes since `/me/bookings` and the member calendar were already scoped to the caller's own bookings. New `MemberMultiSelect` component (`[locale]/calendar/MemberMultiSelect.tsx`) wraps the existing `MemberSearchInput` with removable chips and a lightweight over-capacity warning, used on both the Schedule page's session-creation form and the Activity Types page's schedule-rule form.
  - **Per-gym theming (#51, extended by #68)**: `gyms.theme_key` (migration 030, presets `indigo/emerald/crimson/amber`), `ThemeProvider`, and the superadmin **System → Customize** editor (now replaced by #68).
  - **Centers UI Redesign (#74)**: Centers list now shows Email, truncated Address, Active Members count, and a ContextMenu (⋮) replacing Edit/Delete buttons. Menu items: Edit (existing modal, unchanged), Details (new read-only dialog with General / Statistics / Audit sections including `created_by`/`modified_by`/`deleted_by` resolved names), View Members (navigates to `/members?centerId=X&status=active`), Delete. Migration 064 adds `created_by_membership_id` and `deleted_by_membership_id` to `centers`. `GET /centers` and `GET /centers/:id` return `active_member_count` (subquery) and resolved modifier names via LEFT JOINs. `GET /members` gains `?centerId` filter. Members page reads `centerId` from URL params on load and shows a center dropdown filter (multi-center gyms only). `ContextMenu` component updated to `position: fixed` dropdown so it escapes `overflow: hidden` table containers.
  - **Membership Plans Redesign (#70)**: `membership_plans` gains `lifecycle_status` ENUM(`draft`/`active`/`archived`) + `enrollment_status` ENUM(`open`/`closed`) + soft-delete + audit fields (migrations 058–062). `class_types` renamed to `activity_types` (migration 059, all FK references migrated). New tables: `billing_policies` (1-per-plan billing/service cycle config), `plan_allowances` (replaces `class_type_user_memberships` + `membership_plan_benefits`; supports `unlimited` and `session_count` with recurrence), `membership_plan_centers` (optional per-plan center restriction). API: enriched `GET /membership-plans` (nested `billing_policy`, `allowances`, `centers`, `price_history`, `current_price`, `member_count`); new actions `duplicate`, `archive`, `PUT enrollment`. Booking enforcement (`plan-allowances.ts` side-effect hook) checks center restriction then allowance type/quota. Admin UI replaces DataTable with a per-plan accordion showing all five sections inline; context menu with Edit / Duplicate / Open‑Close Enrollment / Archive / Delete.
  - **Theme Management (#68)**: first-class `themes` entity (migrations 056–057 — table + gym FK). `tokens` JSON carries header/sidebar/typography design tokens. Superadmin **Cordel → Themes** CRUD (draft/active/deleted lifecycle, logo upload up to 512 KB via `POST /platform/themes/:id/logo`, `GET /themes/:id/logo` public caching endpoint). Gyms assigned a theme via `theme_id` FK replacing old `theme_key`. Admin `ThemeProvider` reads `activeGym.theme.tokens` from the gym list response (LEFT JOIN) and writes `--gd-*` CSS variables live on gym switch. Member app introduces `GET /me/gym` (no tenant context needed) so `AppContext` self-resolves gymId + theme; member-side `ThemeProvider` applies the same variables. Both `TopHeader` and `Sidebar` consume `--gd-*` vars; `BottomNav` in the member app follows suit.
  - **Separate Base Themes from Org Themes / Fix Default Logic (#144)**: `GET|PUT|DELETE /platform/themes` now filter to `gym_id IS NULL` — org-created custom themes no longer appear in the Base Themes screen. New `is_system_default TINYINT(1)` column on `themes` (migration 072, Black seeded as initial default) marks the single Base Theme used as factory default when a new organization is created. New endpoint `PUT /platform/themes/:id/set-system-default` atomically flips `is_system_default` to the target theme and clears all others; 404 if the target is not a base theme. Base Themes list response drops `is_default`/`gym_name` in favour of `is_system_default`. UI: Type column always shows "System" badge; Default badge reflects `is_system_default`; "Set as System Default" added to the context menu for non-default base themes. Org default theme logic (`gyms.theme_id`) and center inheritance are unchanged.
  - **Immediate Theme Application on Default Change (#143)**: After a successful `PUT /system/themes/:id/set-default`, the Themes page now calls `refreshGyms()` in parallel with `loadAssignments()`. `GymProvider` re-fetches the gym list (which includes the updated `theme.tokens`), causing `ThemeProvider` to re-apply `--gd-*` CSS variables immediately — no browser refresh required. No backend or migration changes.
  - **Instant Theme Reload on Any Change (#209)**: Any token modification (colors, typography, advanced) is now applied to the DOM immediately via `applyTokens()` when the edited theme is the currently active gym or center theme — no waiting for the debounced save. After the save succeeds, `refreshGyms()` + `refreshCenters()` sync the context so the `ThemeProvider` confirms the new state; on failure the pre-edit tokens are restored. Assignment changes (Assign Centers, Restore Inheritance, Set as Org Default) also call `refreshGyms()` + `refreshCenters()` so the new theme assignment propagates instantly. `CenterOption` in `CenterContext` gains `theme_id: string | null` (already in the API response via `c.*`) to let `isExpandedThemeActive()` detect when the active center has a direct theme override. No backend or migration changes.
  - **Theme Assignment Management (#120)**: Gym admins can now assign themes to the Organization and individual Centers from within the inline Theme editor. System → Themes page (`/themes`) rebuilt as the proper gym-admin page using `GET /system/themes` (was accidentally overwritten in #117 with Base Themes content). New Assignments tab in the inline editor for every theme (base or custom): org-default checkbox (`gyms.theme_id`), center list with Inherited/Assigned badges, Restore Inheritance (sets `centers.theme_id = NULL`), "+ Assign Centers…" multi-select picker, search filter, show-all when > 10. Five new endpoints on `gym-themes.ts`: `GET/:id/assignments`, `PUT/:id/set-default`, `GET/:id/unassigned-centers`, `POST/:id/assign-centers`, `DELETE/:id/centers/:centerId`. Delete guard unified to single message: "This theme is currently in use. Remove all assignments before deleting it." Theme resolution order: `centers.theme_id` → `gyms.theme_id` → first alphabetically.
  - **Base Themes rich list (#121)**: Cordel → Base Themes page gains a rich summary row: Logo (32×32), Name, Description (first line truncated), Type badges (System/Custom + Default when `gyms.theme_id` points to the theme), Usage count (distinct orgs for system themes; centers direct or inherited for custom themes), and Status. The ⋮ context menu now includes a Details action opening a read-only modal with full description, type, status, usage, owner (Cordel / gym name), org-default flag, and audit fields (Created/Modified by + at, resolved from `audit_logs`). New `description TEXT` column added to `themes` (migration 068). `GET /platform/themes` now returns all themes (system + custom) in one list; `GET /platform/themes/:id` includes `created_by_name` / `modified_by_name`.
  - **Meals Sub-Entities UX Alignment (#162)**: Dishes, Sides, and Sauces now follow the platform entity standard. Migration 077 adds `status`, `created_by_membership_id`, `modified_by_membership_id`, `deleted_at`, `deleted_by_membership_id` to all three tables. `DELETE` changed from hard-delete to soft-delete (`status='deleted'`). New `POST /:id/duplicate` endpoint. `GET /` filters out deleted items; `GET /:id` returns full audit fields. Admin UI replaced with `DataTable` with columns: Name, Description (truncated), Created At, Created By, Status badge, Actions. Context menu: Details (read-only modal with audit fields) / Edit / Duplicate / Delete. 65 new tests in `meals-catalog.test.ts`. **Note**: dishes/sides/sauces are fully removed by #163 — `meals-catalog.test.ts` and the three routers are deleted.
  - **Nutrition Library + Nutrition Plan Templates Redesign (#163)**: Replaces the gym-scoped dishes/sides/sauces catalog with a global `nutrition_library_items` table (no `gym_id`, seeded with 32 items across 6 categories: main_dish, side, sauce, drink, dessert, other — same pattern as `benefit_types`). Migration 075 drops `nutrition_plan_template_meal_dishes`, `dishes`, `sides`, `sauces`; creates `nutrition_library_items`, `nutrition_plan_template_restrictions`, `nutrition_plan_template_goals`; adds `main_dish_id`, `side_id`, `sauce_id`, `drink_id` FK columns to `nutrition_plan_template_meals`; extends weekday CHECK to 0–7 (7 = "All Days"). New `nutrition-library.ts` router: `GET /nutrition-library` (global read, optional `?category=` filter, no write endpoints). `nutrition-plan-templates.ts` rewritten: meals now have 4 direct library FK selectors instead of a sortable dish list; new CRUD endpoints for restrictions (`POST/GET/PUT/DELETE /:id/restrictions`) and goals (`POST/GET/PUT/DELETE /:id/goals`); `POST /:id/duplicate` deep-clones days→meals + restrictions + goals in one transaction. `GET /:id/hierarchy` returns `{ days, restrictions, goals }`. Admin: Nutrition Library page (read-only category view), navigation entry renamed from "Meals" to "Nutrition Library", `meals/page.tsx` redirects to `nutrition-library`. `NutritionPlanTree.tsx` rewritten: `MealRow` renders 4 library dropdowns, `RestrictionsSection` and `GoalsSection` appended below the days list. `meals-catalog.ts` router and `meals-catalog.test.ts` deleted. New test files: `nutrition-library.test.ts` (4 tests), `nutrition-plan-templates.test.ts` fully rewritten (removing all dish references).
  - **Nutrition Plan Templates (#132)**: Inline expandable nutrition module. Shared gym-scoped catalogs: `dishes`, `sides`, `sauces` (each a full CRUD router: `GET/POST/PUT/DELETE /dishes|sides|sauces`; `DELETE /dishes/:id` returns 409 if the dish is referenced in any template). Template hierarchy: `nutrition_plan_templates` → days (weekday 0–6, one per weekday per template) → meals (N per day, drag-reorder) → meal_dishes (N per meal, each with optional side + sauce, drag-reorder). New routers: `nutrition-plan-templates.ts` (template CRUD + duplicate deep-clone + hierarchy endpoint + day/meal/meal-dish CRUD + per-level reorder), `meals-catalog.ts` (factory for dishes/sides/sauces). Admin UI: **Nutrition → Meals** page (three expandable catalog sections), **Nutrition → Nutrition Plan Templates** page (expandable cards with inline tree editor using @dnd-kit at three levels). Migration 071 creates 7 new tables. 38 new tests; `cleanupTestGyms` extended with nutrition table deletions.
  - **Centers UX Redesign (#131)**: Centers page replaces the DataTable + modal editor with the standard Cordel inline expandable row pattern. Collapsed row shows Name, Email, Theme (with "(Inherited)" suffix when `center.theme_id` is null), Status inline dropdown, Created At, and a ContextMenu (Details / Delete). Expanding a row opens four inline sections: General Information (Name, Code, Status), Contact Information (Email, Phone), Location (Address), and Theme assignment. All text fields auto-save on blur/Enter; dropdowns (Status, Theme) auto-save on change. No Save/Cancel buttons. "+ Add Center" creates a record via POST then auto-expands + focuses the Name field. Theme section: dropdown of all gym-accessible themes (base + custom); when a direct theme is assigned, a "Restore inheritance" link appears to reset `center.theme_id` to null. Details modal (audit only: Created At/By, Modified At/By, Deleted At/By). The legacy "Edit Center" modal is removed. `GET /centers` and `GET /centers/:id` now join `themes` and `gyms` to return `theme_name` and `gym_theme_name`.
  - **Workout Templates Fully Inline Editing (#130)**: Replaces modal-based block/exercise editing with always-editable inline fields — no save/cancel buttons, no modals. Block header is a single row (name input + type select + rounds input) that auto-saves on blur/change. Exercise table rows are always editable; exercise selection uses a custom filter-as-you-type combobox (position: absolute listbox, closes on outside click). Type-adaptive columns: `reps` → Sets/Min–Max reps/Rest, `time` → Sets/Duration/Rest, `distance` → Distance/Unit/Rest. Rest field displayed in minutes (÷60 display, ×60 on save). New endpoints: `POST /:id/blocks/:blockId/duplicate` (deep-copies block + exercises), `POST /:id/blocks/:blockId/exercises/:exId/duplicate`. Context menus: Duplicate + Delete (no Edit). `BlockModal` and `ExerciseModal` removed. Migration 071 adds `exercise_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'reps'` (CHECK: reps/time/distance) to `exercises`; adds `duration_seconds INT UNSIGNED`, `distance_value DECIMAL(8,2) UNSIGNED`, `distance_unit VARCHAR(20)` to `workout_template_exercises`. `exerciseSummary()` in `summaries.ts` updated for type adaptation and rest-in-minutes.
  - **Promotions UX redesign (#127)**: Promotions page replaces the DataTable + modal editor with the standard inline expandable row pattern. Collapsed row shows Name, Description, Created, Start Date, End Date, Status badge, and a ContextMenu (Details / Duplicate / Delete). Expanding a row opens an inline form for all promotion fields plus two embedded child collections: **Charge Benefits** (charge_type, action, value) and **Period Benefits** (membership_plan, action, value, duration_months). Both collections display as compact tables with inline add-row forms and per-row delete. "+ New Promotion" creates a record and auto-expands + focuses the Name field. Search by name/description with 300ms debounce; status filter; `created_by_membership_id` tracking (migration 069). `promotion_period_benefits` table redesigned from "pay X get Y free" model to "plan + action + value + duration" model (migration 070). New API: `GET /promotions` adds `q`, `created_by`, `sort`, `dir`; `GET /promotions/created-by-options`; `POST /promotions/:id/duplicate` (deep-copies charge + period benefits); `PUT /charge-benefits/:cbId`; `PUT /period-benefits/:pbId`; `POST /period-benefits` updated to new shape. Details modal simplified to audit info only (created_by, created_at). Refined by #158: Edit moved to context menu (rows no longer expand on click), list columns restructured (Type + Value derived from first charge_benefit, Period combines starts/ends, Created By added), Details modal expanded with full promo fields + modified_at/by from audit_logs, `GET /promotions` and `GET /promotions/:id` extended with primary_action_type, primary_value, modified_at, modified_by_name.
  - **Base Themes UX unification (#117)**: Cordel → Base Themes page replaces the DataTable + modal editor with the same inline expandable row pattern used by System → Themes. Each row shows a color swatch, name, status badge, and a context menu (Clone, Details, Delete). Expanding a row opens the branding/typography/colors inline editor. New `POST /platform/themes/clone/:id` clones a base theme into a new base theme (superadmin only). A read-only Details modal surfaces name, created_at, and modified_at. Deleted themes are non-expandable.
  - **Theme Management v2 (#97)**: Separates platform-owned Base Themes from customer-owned Customer Themes. Migration 065 adds `gym_id` to `themes` (NULL = base, non-NULL = customer theme for that gym) and `theme_id` to `centers` (optional per-center override). Seeds "Black" base theme. New `POST /system/themes/clone/:id` (clone any theme to customer theme), `PUT/DELETE /system/themes/:id` (edit/soft-delete customer themes with gym/center conflict guard), logo endpoints. Cordel nav entry renamed to **Base Themes** (`nav.base_themes`); new **System → Themes** page (`/themes`) for gym admins shows System Themes (read-only, expandable) and My Themes (inline editable with ContextMenu: Clone / Activate / Edit / Delete) in separate sections.
  - **Team management (#53)**: gym-scoped admin/coach/staff CRUD via `gym-users.ts` + the **Organization → Team** page. Clerk-invitation flow with an `invited` placeholder row that links on first sign-in; self-edit and last-admin guards; audited. Added `gym_memberships` columns `status`, `email`, `name`, `invitation_id` (migrations 031–034).
  - **Platform superadmin management**: `platform/superadmins` + **System → Users**.
  - **Grouped, role-gated sidebar**: `config/navigationGroups.ts` (Membership / Organization / Training / Nutrition / Financials / System / Cordel).
  - **Staff nav rename (#77)**: Navigation-only refactor. **Team** entry renamed to **Staff** and now points to the existing Trainers page (`/trainers`). The standalone **Trainers** nav entry removed. `trainers.title` i18n key updated to "Staff" across `en`/`es`/`ca` locales. No API, DB, or business logic changes.
- **Placeholder shells (built, content pending)**: per-group Dashboard pages (`organization`, `training`, `nutrition`, `financials`, singular `membership`). Nutrition has no backend yet.
- **Phase 9 — Centers (#59, supersedes Phase 7)**: `Center` (migration 043) as the single
  location concept going forward — `gym_locations` was never built. `member_centers`
  junction (044) gives each Member one or more Centers with a default; `center_id` +
  audit-column backfill added to `rooms`/`class_sessions`/`bookings` (045–047); every
  existing Gym auto-got one default Center named after the gym, every existing Member
  defaulted onto it (046), and new gyms get the same default Center at creation. New
  `resources`/`trainer_availability`/`events` tables (048–050), each `center_id`-scoped
  from creation. `centerContext` middleware (`x-center-id` header) mirrors `tenantContext`;
  `resolveCenterId()` falls back to a gym's sole active Center so single-center gyms need
  no UI changes. Admin: **Organization → Centers/Resources/Events** pages, Trainer
  Availability inline on the Trainers page, Member-edit Assigned Centers/Default Center
  (hidden until a gym has >1 center). Member app: `GET /me/centers` + an optional
  center switcher, hidden for the single-center case. **Phase 7 (#39–#41) is superseded
  and closed** — its `gym_locations`/nullable-FK design was never implemented.
- **Phase 8 — MONEI payments (active, replacing deferred Stripe plan)**: ~~provider abstraction (#179)~~, ~~DB migrations (#180)~~, ~~API routers + isolated payment page token endpoint (#181)~~, ~~admin MemberPaymentsModal (#182)~~, ~~member payment banner + success/error pages (#183)~~, ~~isolated payment page app at `pay.vdicube.com` (#204)~~, ~~infra/Docker for the payment container (#205)~~, ~~recurring MIT billing run (#184)~~, ~~cash payment recording + fiscal receipt (#185)~~, ~~nginx IP allowlist for /billing/ endpoints (#306)~~. The two processes this phase built are documented end to end in `docs/payments.md` (#791).
- Platform naming migrated gymdesk → fitness (2026-07-10/11): DB schema `fitness`, containers/images
  `fitness-*`, VPS user `podman`, env vars `CORDEL_FITNESS_*`. See `docs/architecture.md` § Deployment.
- Legacy cleanup complete: `/fares` and `/subscriptions` routers + the old
  Subscriptions page deleted, `user_memberships.plan` column dropped
  (migrations 004, 007, 009). The member app's `/subscriptions` route was
  renamed to `/membership` (P1.8).
- **#440 (done)**: Staff Management — consolidated the expanded staff profile view in
  `apps/admin/src/app/[locale]/staff/page.tsx` from tabbed sections (General/Employment/
  Schedule/Notes/Clerk) into a single continuous scroll (tab bar + `activeSection` state
  removed). Added multi-center association for Staff, mirroring #59's `member_centers`
  pattern: migration 136 creates `staff_centers` (gym_id/staff_id/center_id, `is_default`,
  same generated-column one-default-per-staff unique index as `member_centers`); migration
  137 backfills it from the old single `staff.assigned_center_id` column (falling back to
  a gym's sole center) and then drops that column — nothing outside `staff.ts` read it.
  New `api/src/api/staff-centers.ts` (`GET`/`PUT /staff/:staffId/centers`, admin-only
  write) mirrors `member-centers.ts`, except a staff member may have zero centers (no
  access-control path depends on staff having one, unlike members). `staff.ts`'s
  create/duplicate now accept/propagate `center_ids`/`default_center_id` via
  `resolveStaffCenters()` (members' `resolveMemberCenters()` equivalent). Frontend adds a
  Centers subsection (checkboxes + default `<select>`, hidden for single-center gyms,
  matching the Members page's `showCenters` pattern) to the consolidated view.
- **#437 (done)**: UI cleanup — removed the admin `[locale]/schedule/` page (and its
  `SessionRosterPanel`/`SharedTrainingPanel` sub-components) plus its nav entry
  (`navigationGroups.ts`'s Calendar group `nav.schedule`/`calendar.schedule` item),
  per the issue's answered clarification that scheduling is now managed from the
  Calendar page rather than a separate section. Session create/edit/cancel/roster
  is already fully covered by the Calendar page's `ClassSessionDetailPanel`
  (unaffected by this change); shared-training request approval
  (`SharedTrainingPanel`'s Approve/Reject UI) has no replacement admin UI yet — see
  `docs/architecture.md`'s "Planned: CalendarEvent Unification" (#360 stage 4) note.
  Removed the `nav.schedule` key and the entire top-level `schedule.*` i18n block from
  `apps/admin/locales/base/{en,ca,es}.json`. No backend/API changes — `bookings.ts` and
  `shared-training-requests.ts` are untouched.
- **#481 (done)**: Eligible Membership Plans + Public Event on Activity Types —
  migration 139 adds `activity_types.public_event TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1`
  (defaults true for backward compatibility — every pre-existing/zero-config
  activity type stays bookable by anyone) and join table
  `activity_type_eligible_plans` (gym_id/activity_type_id/membership_plan_id,
  inverse semantics of `membership_plan_centers`: the join table only gates
  bookings when `public_event=false`). New `GET`/`PUT /activity-types/:id/eligible-plans`
  (replace-all, mirrors `spaces.ts`'s `/:id/activity-types`). New
  `api/src/api/activity-eligibility.ts` registers a `registerBookingAccessHook`
  enforcing this at booking time — then independent from `plan-allowances`/
  `package-credits` entitlement checks and registered before both in `app.ts`;
  since #635 stage 4 it is the only plan-based gate and claims the class-package
  credit itself.
  Added a generic staff-override mechanism spanning both hook families:
